// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// LOYALTY SYSTEM
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const LOY_TIERS ={
  bronze:{label:'برونزي', icon:'💎', color:'#cd7f32', min:0},
  silver:{label:'فضي',    icon:'🥈', color:'#94a3b8', min:500},
  gold:{label:'ذهبي',   icon:'🥇', color:'#EBA33E', min:1500}
};
const TRANSFER_AMOUNT     = 1200;   // Default monthly transfer amount (SAR)
const COMMISSION_AMOUNT   = 300;    // Default agent commission (SAR)
const IQAMA_WARN_DAYS     = 30;     // Days before iqama expiry to warn
const PASSPORT_WARN_DAYS  = 60;     // Days before passport expiry to warn
const MAX_UNDO_HISTORY    = 30;     // Max undo stack depth
const BROKER_ALERT_DAYS   = 7;      // Days before entitlement due to alert
const BACKUP_MAX_COUNT    = 10;     // Max stored backups
const SEARCH_DEBOUNCE_MS  = 300;    // Search input debounce milliseconds
const MAX_OPS_LOG         = 200;    // Max activity log entries displayed
const MIN_NAME_LENGTH     = 2;      // Minimum characters for a valid name
function getTier(points){
function escHtml(str){
  if (!str) return '';
  return String(str)
  .replace(/&/g,'&amp;')
  .replace(/</g,'&lt;')
  .replace(/>/g,'&gt;')
  .replace(/"/g,'&quot;')
  .replace(/'/g,'&#39;');
}
function isValidPhone(phone){
  if (!phone) return true; // phone is optional in most forms
  return /^05\d{8}$/.test(String(phone).replace(/\s/g,''));
}
function validateRequired(fields){
  for (const [label, value] of Object.entries(fields)){
  if (!value || String(value).trim().length < MIN_NAME_LENGTH){
  return label + ' مطلوب ويجب أن يكون أكثر من حرف واحد';
  }
  }
  return null;
}
  if(points >= 1500) return 'gold';
  if(points >= 500)  return 'silver';
  return 'bronze';
}
function addLoyalty(){
  const name  = document.getElementById('loy-name')?.value.trim();
  const phone = document.getElementById('loy-phone')?.value.trim();
  const id    = document.getElementById('loy-id')?.value.trim()||'';
  const tier  = document.getElementById('loy-tier')?.value||'bronze';
  const date  = document.getElementById('loy-date')?.value||today();
  const pts   = parseInt(document.getElementById('loy-points')?.value||'0')||0;
  const note  = document.getElementById('loy-note')?.value.trim()||'';
  if(!name||!phone){ showToast('⚠️ الاسم والجوال مطلوبان','#D9534F'); return; }
  if(!data.loyalty) data.loyalty=[];
  data.loyalty.push({ name, phone, id, tier, points:pts, date, note, history:[] });
  saveData();
  closeModal('m-add-loyalty');
  ['loy-name','loy-phone','loy-id','loy-points','loy-note'].forEach(f=>{const el=document.getElementById(f);if(el)el.value='';});
  showToast('✅ تم تسجيل '+name+' في نظام الولاء');
  renderLoyalty();
}
function addLoyaltyPoints(idx, pts){
  if(!data.loyalty[idx]) return;
  const p = parseInt(pts)||0;
  data.loyalty[idx].points = Math.max(0, (data.loyalty[idx].points||0) + p);
  data.loyalty[idx].tier   = getTier(data.loyalty[idx].points);
  data.loyalty[idx].history = data.loyalty[idx].history||[];
  data.loyalty[idx].history.push({ date:today(), pts:p, note:'إضافة يدوية' });
  saveData();
  renderLoyalty();
  showToast((p>0?'✅ أضيف ':'⚠️ خُصم ')+ Math.abs(p)+' نقطة');
}
function delLoyalty(idx){
  if(!confirm('حذف هذا العميل من نظام الولاء؟')) return;
  data.loyalty.splice(idx,1);
  saveData();
  renderLoyalty();
  showToast('🗑️ تم الحذف','#D9534F');
}
function renderLoyalty(){
  if(!data.loyalty) data.loyalty=[];
  const search = (document.getElementById('loy-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  let list = data.loyalty.map((c,i)=>({...c,_i:i}));
  if(search) list = list.filter(c=> c.name.toLowerCase().includes(search)||c.phone.includes(search));
  const set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('loy-total',  data.loyalty.length);
  set('loy-bronze', data.loyalty.filter(c=>getTier(c.points)==='bronze').length);
  set('loy-silver', data.loyalty.filter(c=>getTier(c.points)==='silver').length);
  set('loy-gold',   data.loyalty.filter(c=>getTier(c.points)==='gold').length);
  const grid = document.getElementById('loyalty-container');
  if(!grid) return;
  if(!list.length){
  grid.innerHTML='<div class="empty" style="grid-column:1/-1">لا يوجد عملاء في نظام الولاء</div>';
  return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = list.map(c=>{
  const t = LOY_TIERS[getTier(c.points)];
  const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((c.points / Math.max(1500,c.points+100))*100));
  return `<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;transition:all 0.2s"
  onmouseover="this.style.borderColor='#F9B264';this.style.transform='translateY(-2px)'"
  onmouseout="this.style.borderColor='var(--line)';this.style.transform=''">
  <div style="height:4px;background:${t.color}"></div>
  <div style="padding:16px">
  <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:12px">
  <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
  <div style="width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:50%;background:${t.color}22;border:2px solid ${t.color}; display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:20px">${t.icon}</div>
  <div>
  <div style="font-weight:900;font-size:14px">${c.name}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">${c.phone}</div>
  </div>
  </div>
  <span style="background:${t.color}22;color:${t.color};border:1px solid ${t.color}55; border-radius:20px;padding:3px 10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">${t.label}</span>
  </div>
  <div style="margin-bottom:12px">
  <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3);margin-bottom:4px">
  <span>النقاط</span><span style="color:${t.color};font-weight:900">${c.points} نقطة</span>
  </div>
  <div style="background:var(--bg);border-radius:6px;height:6px;overflow:hidden">
  <div style="width:${pct}%;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,${t.color},${t.color}99);transition:width 0.5s"></div>
  </div>
  </div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3);margin-bottom:12px">
  ${c.id?'🪪 '+c.id+'<br>':''} 📅 مسجل: ${c.date}
  ${c.note?'<br>📝 '+c.note:''}
  </div>
  <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">
  <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" style="flex:1;justify-content:center"
  onclick="const p=prompt('أدخل النقاط (سالب للخصم):');if(p!==null)addLoyaltyPoints(${c._i},parseInt(p)||0)">
  ➕ نقاط</button>
  <a href="tel:${c.phone}" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="text-decoration:none;justify-content:center">📞</a>
  <a href="https://wa.me/966${c.phone.replace(/^0/,'')}" target="_blank"
  class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="text-decoration:none;justify-content:center">💬</a>
  <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delLoyalty(${c._i})">🗑️</button>
  </div>
  </div>
  </div>`;
  }).join('');
}
function exportLoyaltyCSV(){
  if(!data.loyalty||!data.loyalty.length){ showToast('لا يوجد بيانات للتصدير','#D9534F'); return; }
  const rows=[['الاسم','الجوال','الهوية','المستوى','النقاط','تاريخ التسجيل','ملاحظة']];
  data.loyalty.forEach(c=>rows.push([c.name,c.phone,c.id||'',LOY_TIERS[getTier(c.points)].label,c.points,c.date,c.note||'']));
  const csv=rows.map(r=>r.join(',')).join('\n');
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(['\ufeff'+csv],{type:'text/csv'}));
  a.download='نظام_الولاء.csv'; a.click();
}
const STORE_KEY = 'hulul_v4';
let data ={ workers:[], sponsors:[], transfers:[], accounting:[], sales:[], expensesList:[], inventory:[], saudiEmployees:[], customers:[], attendance:[], files:[], ops:[] };
let history_stack = [], redo_stack = [];
function saveData(){ try{ localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data)); } catch(e){} }
function loadData(){ try{ const d = localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY); if(d) data ={...data, ...JSON.parse(d)}; } catch(e){} }
function snapshot(label){ history_stack.push({label, state: JSON.stringify(data)}); if(history_stack.length > 30) history_stack.shift(); redo_stack = []; updateUndoBtn(); }
function undo(){ if(!history_stack.length) return; const snap = history_stack.pop(); redo_stack.push({label: snap.label, state: JSON.stringify(data)}); data = JSON.parse(snap.state); saveData(); refreshCurrentSection(); updateUndoBtn(); showToast('↩️ تم التراجع عن: ' + snap.label); }
function updateUndoBtn(){ (function(){const _e=document.getElementById('undo-label');if(_e)_e.textContent=history_stack.length ? history_stack[history_stack.length-1].label : 'لا يوجد';}()); document.getElementById('undo-count').textContent = history_stack.length; }
function addOp(text){ data.ops.unshift({text, date: new Date().toLocaleString('ar-SA')}); if(data.ops.length > 50) data.ops = data.ops.slice(0,50); }
function today(){ return new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]; }
function fmt(n){ return Number(n||0).toLocaleString('ar-SA',{maximumFractionDigits:2}); }
const SECTIONS = ['dashboard','workers','transfers','accounting','sales','expenses','inventory','saudi','contacts','loyalty','attendance','files','tasks','receipts','brokers'];
let currentSection = 'dashboard';
function refreshCurrentSection(){ showSec(currentSection); }
function switchDash(panel){
  ['overview','accounting','sales','expenses','inventory'].forEach(p=>{
  document.getElementById('dash-'+p).style.display = p===panel?'block':'none';
  const btn=document.getElementById('subbtn-'+p);
  if(btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', p===panel);
  });
  if(panel==='overview') renderOverview();
  if(panel==='accounting') renderAccounting();
  if(panel==='sales') renderSales();
  if(panel==='expenses') renderExpenses();
  if(panel==='inventory') renderInventory();
}
function switchWorkers(panel){
  ['workers','sponsors','transfers','schedule'].forEach(p=>{
  document.getElementById('wpanel-'+p).style.display = p===panel?'block':'none';
  btn=document.getElementById('wsubbtn-'+p);
  if(btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', p===panel);
  });
  if(panel==='workers') renderWorkers();
  if(panel==='sponsors') renderSponsors();
  if(panel==='transfers') renderTransfers('all');
  if(panel==='schedule') renderCal();
}
let activeConFilter='all';
function switchContacts(type){
  activeConFilter=type;
  ['all','workers','sponsors','offices','customers','loyalty'].forEach(t=>{
  btn=document.getElementById('csubbtn-'+t);
  if(btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', t===type);
  });
  const lp=document.getElementById('loyalty-container');
  const cg=document.getElementById('contacts-grid');
  const kpis=document.getElementById('con-kpis');
  const searchRow=document.getElementById('con-search-row');
  if(type==='loyalty'){
  if(lp) lp.style.display='block';
  if(cg) cg.style.display='none';
  if(kpis) kpis.style.display='none';
  if(searchRow) searchRow.style.display='none';
  renderLoyalty();
  } else{
  if(lp) lp.style.display='none';
  if(cg) cg.style.display='';
  if(kpis) kpis.style.display='';
  if(searchRow) searchRow.style.display='';
  renderContacts();
  }
}

  document.getElementById('apanel-'+p).style.display=p===panel?'block':'none';
  btn=document.getElementById('asubbtn-'+p);
  if(btn) btn.classList.toggle('active', p===panel);
  });
  if(panel==='daily') renderAttendance();
  if(panel==='report') renderAttReport();
  if(panel==='history') renderAttHistory();
}
function openModal(id){
  el=document.getElementById(id);
  if(el) el.classList.add('open');
  if(id==='m-add-worker'){ populateWorkerSponsorSelect(); populateSponsorSelects(); }
  if(id==='m-add-transfer') populateTransferWorkerSelect();
  if(id==='m-add-sale'){
  const dl = document.getElementById('loyalty-datalist');
  if(dl && data.loyalty){
  dl.innerHTML = (data.loyalty||[]).map(c=>`<option value="${c.name}">${c.name} — ${c.phone} — ${c.points||0} نقطة</option>`).join('');
  }
  }
  el.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]').forEach(i=>{ if(!i.value) i.value=today(); });
}
function closeModal(id){ const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.classList.remove('open'); }
document.addEventListener('click', e=>{ if(e.target.classList.contains('modal-overlay')) e.target.classList.remove('open'); });
let toastTimer;
function renderOverview(){
  const allIncome = data.accounting.filter(e=>e.type==='income').reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0)
  + data.sales.reduce((s,s2)=>s+s2.qty*s2.price,0);
  const allExp = data.accounting.filter(e=>e.type==='expense').reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0)
  + data.expensesList.reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0);
  const netProfit = allIncome - allExp;
  const thisMonth = today().slice(0,7);
  const monthSales = data.sales.filter(s=>(s.date||'').startsWith(thisMonth)).reduce((s,e)=>s+e.qty*e.price,0);
  const allPay = [
  ...data.sales.map(s=>({m:s.payMethod||'كاش', v:s.qty*s.price})),
  ...data.accounting.filter(e=>e.type==='income').map(e=>({m:e.payMethod||'كاش', v:e.amount}))
  ];
  const cash = allPay.filter(p=>p.m==='كاش').reduce((s,p)=>s+p.v,0);
  const bank = allPay.filter(p=>p.m==='تحويل').reduce((s,p)=>s+p.v,0);
  const pos  = allPay.filter(p=>p.m==='شبكة').reduce((s,p)=>s+p.v,0);
  const maxP = Math.max(cash,bank,pos,1);
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('kpi-income',  fmt(allIncome)+' ر.س');
  set('kpi-expenses',fmt(allExp)+' ر.س');
  set('kpi-profit',  fmt(netProfit)+' ر.س');
  set('kpi-month-sales', fmt(monthSales)+' ر.س');
  set('kpi-cash',  fmt(cash)+' ر.س');
  set('kpi-bank',  fmt(bank)+' ر.س');
  set('kpi-pos',   fmt(pos)+' ر.س');
  set('kpi-inv-count', (data.inventory||[]).length);
  const bar=(id,val)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.style.width=Math.round(val/maxP*100)+'%';};
  bar('pmbar-cash',cash); set('pmval-cash',fmt(cash)+' ر.س');
  bar('pmbar-bank',bank); set('pmval-bank',fmt(bank)+' ر.س');
  bar('pmbar-pos',pos);   set('pmval-pos',fmt(pos)+' ر.س');
  const todayT = data.transfers.filter(t=>t.dueDate===today()&&t.status!=='done').length;
  const sauAl  = (data.saudiEmployees||[]).filter(e=>{if(!e.excludeDate)return false;const d=new Date(e.excludeDate);const now=new Date();return (d-now)/(1000*3600*24)<=30;}).length;
  const alertDiv=document.getElementById('dash-kpi-alert');
  if(alertDiv){
  if(todayT>0||sauAl>0){alertDiv.style.display='flex';let t='';if(todayT)t+=todayT+' حوالة مستحقة اليوم';if(sauAl)t+=(t?' | ':'')+sauAl+' موظف يقترب موعد استبعاده';document.getElementById('dash-kpi-alert-text').textContent=t;}
  else alertDiv.style.display='none';
  }
  const lowItems=(data.inventory||[]).filter(i=>i.qty<=i.min);
  const invAlerts=document.getElementById('inv-alerts');
  if(invAlerts){
  if(!lowItems.length){invAlerts.innerHTML='<div class="empty">لا توجد تنبيهات مخزون</div>';}
  else invAlerts.innerHTML=lowItems.map(i=>'<div class="timeline-item"><div class="timeline-dot" style="background:var(--red)"></div><div><strong>'+i.name+'</strong> <span class="badge badge-red">'+i.qty+' '+( i.unit||'')+'</span> — الحد الأدنى: '+i.min+'</div></div>').join('');
  }
  const opsDiv=document.getElementById('dash-ops-list');
  if(opsDiv){
  if(!data.ops.length)opsDiv.innerHTML='<div class="empty">لا توجد عمليات</div>';
  else opsDiv.innerHTML=data.ops.slice(0,12).map(op=>'<div class="timeline-item"><div class="timeline-dot gold"></div><div><div style="font-size:13px">'+op.text+'</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">'+op.date+'</div></div></div>').join('');
  }
  renderChart();
  const loy = data.loyalty||[];
  const setL=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  setL('dash-loy-total',  loy.length);
  setL('dash-loy-bronze', loy.filter(c=>getTier(c.points||0)==='bronze').length);
  setL('dash-loy-silver', loy.filter(c=>getTier(c.points||0)==='silver').length);
  setL('dash-loy-gold',   loy.filter(c=>getTier(c.points||0)==='gold').length);
}
function renderChart(){
  const canvas=document.getElementById('chart-monthly');
  if(!canvas)return;
  const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
  canvas.width=canvas.offsetWidth||400; canvas.height=200;
  const W=canvas.width; const H=170;
  const months=[];
  const now=new Date();
  for(let i=5;i>=0;i--){const d=new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth()-i,1);months.push(d.getFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'));}
  const incArr=months.map(m=>data.accounting.filter(e=>e.type==='income'&&(e.date||'').startsWith(m)).reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0)+data.sales.filter(s=>(s.date||'').startsWith(m)).reduce((s,e)=>s+e.qty*e.price,0));
  const expArr=months.map(m=>data.expensesList.filter(e=>(e.date||'').startsWith(m)).reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0)+data.accounting.filter(e=>e.type==='expense'&&(e.date||'').startsWith(m)).reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0));
  const maxV=Math.max(...incArr,...expArr,1);
  ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,200);
  const bW=Math.max(10,Math.floor(W/months.length/3));
  const gW=Math.floor(W/months.length);
  months.forEach((m,i)=>{
  const x=i*gW+gW*0.15;
  const iH=Math.max(2,(incArr[i]/maxV)*(H-20));
  const eH=Math.max(2,(expArr[i]/maxV)*(H-20));
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,201,167,0.8)';ctx.beginPath();
  if(ctx.roundRect)ctx.roundRect(x,H-iH,bW,iH,[3,3,0,0]);else ctx.rect(x,H-iH,bW,iH);ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,77,109,0.8)';ctx.beginPath();
  if(ctx.roundRect)ctx.roundRect(x+bW+3,H-eH,bW,eH,[3,3,0,0]);else ctx.rect(x+bW+3,H-eH,bW,eH);ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle='#6b7a99';ctx.font='10px Cairo';ctx.textAlign='center';
  ctx.fillText(m.split('-')[1]+'/'+m.split('-')[0].slice(2),x+bW,192);
  });
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,201,167,0.8)';ctx.fillRect(12,8,14,8);
  ctx.fillStyle='#e8eaf0';ctx.font='11px Cairo';ctx.textAlign='right';ctx.fillText('إيرادات',W-30,16);
  ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,77,109,0.8)';ctx.fillRect(W-80,8,14,8);
  ctx.fillStyle='#e8eaf0';ctx.fillText('نفقات',W-90,16);
}
function addIncome(){
  const desc=document.getElementById('inc-desc').value.trim();
  const amount=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inc-amount').value)||0;
  const method=document.getElementById('inc-method').value;
  const cat=document.getElementById('inc-cat').value;
  date=document.getElementById('inc-date').value||today();
  const ref=document.getElementById('inc-ref').value.trim();
  if(!desc||!amount){showToast('⚠️ الوصف والمبلغ مطلوبان','#D9534F');return;}
  snapshot('إضافة إيراد: '+desc);
  data.accounting.push({type:'income',desc,amount,payMethod:method,cat,date,ref,addedDate:today()});
  addOp('إيراد: '+desc+' — '+fmt(amount)+' ر.س');
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-income'); showToast('✅ تم إضافة الإيراد');
  renderAccounting(); updateHeaderStats();
}
function addExpenseAcc(){
  name=document.getElementById('exp-name').value.trim();
  amount=parseFloat(document.getElementById('exp-amount').value)||0;
  cat=document.getElementById('exp-cat').value;
  method=document.getElementById('exp-method').value;
  const vendor=document.getElementById('exp-vendor').value.trim();
  const invoice=document.getElementById('exp-invoice')?.value.trim();
  date=document.getElementById('exp-date').value||today();
  note=document.getElementById('exp-note').value.trim();
  if(!name||!amount||!cat||!method){showToast('⚠️ يرجى ملء الحقول المطلوبة','#D9534F');return;}
  snapshot('إضافة نفقة: '+name);
  data.expensesList.push({name,amount,cat,payMethod:method,vendor,invoice,date,note,addedDate:today()});
  addOp('نفقة: '+name+' — '+fmt(amount)+' ر.س');
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-expense'); showToast('✅ تم إضافة النفقة');
  renderExpenses(); updateHeaderStats();
}
function addExpense(){addExpenseAcc();}
function renderAccounting(){
  const typeF=document.getElementById('acc-filter-type')?.value||'';
  const methodF=document.getElementById('acc-filter-method')?.value||'';
  const monthF=document.getElementById('acc-filter-month')?.value||'';
  rows=[...data.accounting];
  if(typeF) rows=rows.filter(r=>r.type===typeF);
  if(methodF) rows=rows.filter(r=>r.payMethod===methodF);
  if(monthF) rows=rows.filter(r=>(r.date||'').startsWith(monthF));
  rows=rows.sort((a,b)=>b.date?.localeCompare(a.date||'')||0);
  const totalInc=data.accounting.filter(r=>r.type==='income').reduce((s,r)=>s+r.amount,0);
  totalExp=data.accounting.filter(r=>r.type==='expense').reduce((s,r)=>s+r.amount,0);
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('acc-total-income',fmt(totalInc)+' ر.س');
  set('acc-total-expense',fmt(totalExp)+' ر.س');
  set('acc-net',fmt(totalInc-totalExp)+' ر.س');
  const tbody=document.getElementById('accounting-tbody');
  if(!tbody)return;
  if(!rows.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty">لا توجد سجلات</td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML=rows.map((r,ri)=>{
  const i=data.accounting.indexOf(r);
  const isInc=r.type==='income';
  return '<tr><td>'+(ri+1)+'</td><td>'+r.date+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(isInc?'badge-teal':'badge-red')+'">'+(isInc?'💰 إيراد':'📤 مصروف')+'</span></td><td>'+r.desc+'</td><td style="font-weight:700;color:'+(isInc?'#0d7a6a':'#D9534F')+'">'+fmt(r.amount)+' ر.س</td><td>'+(r.payMethod||'—')+'</td><td>'+(r.cat||'—')+'</td><td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">'+(r.ref||'—')+'</td><td><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delAccounting('+i+')">🗑️</button></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}
function delAccounting(i){if(!confirm('حذف؟'))return;snapshot('حذف قيد');data.accounting.splice(i,1);saveData();renderAccounting();updateHeaderStats();}
function calcSaleTotals(){
  const qty=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-qty')?.value)||0;
  const price=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-price')?.value)||0;
  const cost=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-cost')?.value)||0;
  const total=qty*price; const costBulk=qty*cost;
  const profit=total-costBulk;
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  const setV=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value=v;};
  set('sal-total-val',fmt(total)+' ر.س');
  set('sal-cost-val',fmt(costBulk)+' ر.س');
  set('sal-profit-val',fmt(profit)+' ر.س');
  setV('sal-cost-bulk',fmt(costBulk)+' ر.س');
}
function addSale(){
  name=document.getElementById('sal-name').value.trim();
  qty=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-qty').value)||0;
  price=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-price').value)||0;
  cost=parseFloat(document.getElementById('sal-cost').value)||0;
  cat=document.getElementById('sal-cat').value;
  method=document.getElementById('sal-method').value;
  vendor=document.getElementById('sal-vendor')?.value.trim();
  const vendorPhone=document.getElementById('sal-vendor-phone')?.value.trim();
  const client=document.getElementById('sal-client').value.trim();
  date=document.getElementById('sal-date').value||today();
  invoice=document.getElementById('sal-invoice')?.value.trim();
  if(!name||!qty||!price){showToast('⚠️ الاسم والكمية والسعر مطلوبة','#D9534F');return;}
  snapshot('إضافة مبيعة: '+name);
  data.sales.push({name,qty,price,cost,cat,payMethod:method,vendor,vendorPhone,client,date,invoice,addedDate:today()});
  addOp('مبيعة: '+name+' — '+fmt(qty*price)+' ر.س');
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-sale'); showToast('✅ تم إضافة المبيعة');
  if(client && data.loyalty){
  const li = data.loyalty.findIndex(c=>c.name===client||c.phone===client);
  if(li>=0){
  pts = Math.floor((qty*price)/100)*10;
  if(pts>0){
  data.loyalty[li].points=(data.loyalty[li].points||0)+pts;
  data.loyalty[li].tier=getTier(data.loyalty[li].points);
  data.loyalty[li].history=data.loyalty[li].history||[];
  data.loyalty[li].history.push({date:today(),pts,note:'مبيعة: '+name});
  saveData(); showToast('🌟 '+pts+' نقطة ولاء للعميل '+data.loyalty[li].name);
  }
  }
  }
  renderSales(); updateHeaderStats();
}
function renderSales(){
  search=(document.getElementById('sal-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  const catF=document.getElementById('sal-filter-cat')?.value||'';
  monthF=document.getElementById('sal-filter-month')?.value||'';
  rows=[...data.sales];
  if(search)rows=rows.filter(r=>r.name.toLowerCase().includes(search)||(r.client||'').toLowerCase().includes(search));
  if(catF)rows=rows.filter(r=>r.cat===catF);
  if(monthF)rows=rows.filter(r=>(r.date||'').startsWith(monthF));
  rows=rows.sort((a,b)=>b.date?.localeCompare(a.date)||0);
  const totSales=data.sales.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.qty*r.price,0);
  const totCost=data.sales.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.qty*r.cost,0);
  const totProfit=totSales-totCost;
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('sal-total',fmt(totSales)+' ر.س');
  set('sal-profit',fmt(totProfit)+' ر.س');
  set('sal-count',data.sales.length);
  set('sal-cost',fmt(totCost)+' ر.س');
  tbody=document.getElementById('sales-tbody');
  if(!tbody)return;
  if(!rows.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="14" class="empty">لا توجد مبيعات</td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML=rows.map((r,ri)=>{
  i=data.sales.indexOf(r);
  total=r.qty*r.price; const costBulk=r.qty*r.cost; const profit=total-costBulk;
  return '<tr><td>'+(ri+1)+'</td><td>'+r.date+'</td><td><strong>'+r.name+'</strong></td><td><span class="badge badge-blue">'+r.cat+'</span></td><td>'+r.qty+'</td><td>'+fmt(r.price)+' ر.س</td><td>'+fmt(r.cost)+' ر.س</td><td><strong>'+fmt(total)+' ر.س</strong></td><td style="color:'+(profit>=0?'#0d7a6a':'#D9534F')+'">'+fmt(profit)+' ر.س</td><td>'+(r.payMethod||'—')+'</td><td style="font-size:11px">'+(r.vendor||'—')+'</td><td style="font-size:11px">'+(r.client||'—')+'</td><td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">'+(r.invoice||'—')+'</td><td><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delSale('+i+')">🗑️</button></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}
function delSale(i){if(!confirm('حذف؟'))return;snapshot('حذف مبيعة');data.sales.splice(i,1);saveData();renderSales();updateHeaderStats();}
function renderExpenses(){
  search=(document.getElementById('exp-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  catF=document.getElementById('exp-filter-cat')?.value||'';
  methodF=document.getElementById('exp-filter-method')?.value||'';
  monthF=document.getElementById('exp-filter-month')?.value||'';
  rows=[...data.expensesList];
  if(search)rows=rows.filter(r=>r.name.toLowerCase().includes(search));
  if(catF)rows=rows.filter(r=>r.cat===catF);
  if(methodF)rows=rows.filter(r=>r.payMethod===methodF);
  if(monthF)rows=rows.filter(r=>(r.date||'').startsWith(monthF));
  rows=rows.sort((a,b)=>b.date?.localeCompare(a.date)||0);
  thisMonth=today().slice(0,7);
  const tot=data.expensesList.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.amount,0);
  const monTot=data.expensesList.filter(r=>(r.date||'').startsWith(thisMonth)).reduce((s,r)=>s+r.amount,0);
  const maxR=data.expensesList.reduce((mx,r)=>Math.max(mx,r.amount),0);
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('exp-total',fmt(tot)+' ر.س'); set('exp-month',fmt(monTot)+' ر.س'); set('exp-count',data.expensesList.length); set('exp-max',fmt(maxR)+' ر.س');
  const cats={};data.expensesList.forEach(r=>{cats[r.cat]=(cats[r.cat]||0)+r.amount;});
  const catsDiv=document.getElementById('exp-cats');
  if(catsDiv)catsDiv.innerHTML=Object.entries(cats).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([cat,am])=>'<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 14px;font-size:12px"><strong>'+cat+'</strong><div style="color:var(--red);font-weight:900">'+fmt(am)+' ر.س</div></div>').join('');
  tbody=document.getElementById('expenses-tbody');
  if(!tbody)return;
  if(!rows.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="10" class="empty">لا توجد نفقات</td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML=rows.map((r,ri)=>{
  i=data.expensesList.indexOf(r);
  return '<tr><td>'+(ri+1)+'</td><td>'+r.date+'</td><td><strong>'+r.name+'</strong></td><td><span class="badge badge-red">'+r.cat+'</span></td><td style="color:var(--red);font-weight:700">'+fmt(r.amount)+' ر.س</td><td>'+(r.payMethod||'—')+'</td><td style="font-size:11px">'+(r.vendor||'—')+'</td><td style="font-size:11px">'+(r.invoice||'—')+'</td><td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">'+(r.note||'—')+'</td><td><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delExpense('+i+')">🗑️</button></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}
function delExpense(i){if(!confirm('حذف؟'))return;snapshot('حذف نفقة');data.expensesList.splice(i,1);saveData();renderExpenses();updateHeaderStats();}
function calcInv(){
  qty=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inv-qty')?.value)||0;
  const buy=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inv-buy')?.value)||0;
  const sell=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inv-sell')?.value)||0;
  const vEl=document.getElementById('inv-val'); if(vEl)vEl.textContent=fmt(qty*buy)+' ر.س';
  const mEl=document.getElementById('inv-margin'); if(mEl){const m=buy>0?Math.round((sell-buy)/buy*100):0;mEl.textContent=m+'%';mEl.style.color=m>=0?'#0d7a6a':'#D9534F';}
}
function addInventory(){
  name=document.getElementById('inv-name').value.trim();
  const sku=document.getElementById('inv-sku').value.trim();
  cat=document.getElementById('inv-cat').value;
  const unit=document.getElementById('inv-unit').value.trim();
  qty=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inv-qty').value)||0;
  const min=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inv-min').value)||5;
  buy=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inv-buy').value)||0;
  sell=parseFloat(document.getElementById('inv-sell').value)||0;
  note=document.getElementById('inv-note')?.value.trim();
  if(!name){showToast('⚠️ أدخل اسم الصنف','#D9534F');return;}
  snapshot('إضافة جرد: '+name);
  data.inventory.push({name,sku,cat,unit,qty,min,buy,sell,note,addedDate:today()});
  addOp('صنف جديد في الجرد: '+name);
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-inventory'); showToast('✅ تم إضافة الصنف: '+name);
  renderInventory();
}
function renderInventory(){
  search=(document.getElementById('inv-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  catF=document.getElementById('inv-filter-cat')?.value||'';
  const statusF=document.getElementById('inv-filter-status')?.value||'';
  rows=[...data.inventory];
  if(search)rows=rows.filter(r=>r.name.toLowerCase().includes(search)||(r.sku||'').toLowerCase().includes(search));
  if(catF)rows=rows.filter(r=>r.cat===catF);
  if(statusF==='low')rows=rows.filter(r=>r.qty<=r.min);
  if(statusF==='ok')rows=rows.filter(r=>r.qty>r.min);
  const totVal=data.inventory.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.qty*r.buy,0);
  const lowCnt=data.inventory.filter(r=>r.qty<=r.min).length;
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('inv-total-count',data.inventory.length); set('inv-total-val',fmt(totVal)+' ر.س'); set('inv-low-count',lowCnt); set('inv-ok-count',data.inventory.length-lowCnt);
  tbody=document.getElementById('inventory-tbody');
  if(!tbody)return;
  if(!rows.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="12" class="empty">لا توجد أصناف</td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML=rows.map((r,ri)=>{
  i=data.inventory.indexOf(r);
  const isLow=r.qty<=r.min;
  const margin=r.buy>0?Math.round((r.sell-r.buy)/r.buy*100):0;
  const status=r.qty===0?'<span class="badge badge-red">نفذ</span>':isLow?'<span class="badge" style="background:rgba(249,115,22,0.15);color:#e08800">منخفض</span>':'<span class="badge badge-teal">متاح</span>';
  return '<tr style="'+(isLow?'background:rgba(255,77,109,0.04)':'')+'"><td>'+(ri+1)+'</td><td><code>'+(r.sku||'—')+'</code></td><td><strong>'+r.name+'</strong></td><td>'+r.cat+'</td><td style="font-weight:700;color:'+(isLow?'#D9534F':'#0d7a6a')+'">'+r.qty+' '+(r.unit||'')+'</td><td style="color:var(--ink3)">'+r.min+'</td><td>'+fmt(r.buy)+' ر.س</td><td>'+fmt(r.sell)+' ر.س</td><td><strong>'+fmt(r.qty*r.buy)+' ر.س</strong></td><td style="color:'+(margin>=0?'#0d7a6a':'#D9534F')+'">'+margin+'%</td><td>'+status+'</td><td style="display:flex;gap:4px"><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="editInvQty('+i+')">📝</button><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delInv('+i+')">🗑️</button></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}
function editInvQty(i){const r=data.inventory[i];const q=prompt('الكمية الجديدة لـ "'+r.name+'":', r.qty);if(q===null)return;snapshot('تعديل كمية: '+r.name);data.inventory[i].qty=parseFloat(q)||0;saveData();renderInventory();showToast('✅ تم التحديث');}
function delInv(i){if(!confirm('حذف هذا الصنف؟'))return;snapshot('حذف جرد');data.inventory.splice(i,1);saveData();renderInventory();}
function populateWorkerSponsorSelect(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('wk-sponsor');
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">-- اختر الكفيل --</option>';
  data.sponsors.forEach((s,i)=>sel.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<option value="'+i+'">'+s.name+'</option>'));
}
function populateTransferWorkerSelect(){
  sel=document.getElementById('tr-worker');
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">-- اختر العامل --</option>';
  data.workers.forEach((w,i)=>sel.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<option value="'+i+'">'+w.name+'</option>'));
}
function addWorker(){
  const name=document.getElementById('wk-name')?.value.trim()||'';
  const id=document.getElementById('wk-id')?.value.trim()||'';
  const phone=document.getElementById('wk-phone')?.value.trim()||'';
  const nat=document.getElementById('wk-nat')?.value||'';
  const marketer=document.getElementById('wk-marketer')?.value.trim()||'';
  const sponsorIdxVal=document.getElementById('wk-sponsor')?.value;
  const date=document.getElementById('wk-date')?.value||today();
  const note=document.getElementById('wk-note')?.value.trim()||'';
  if(!name||!nat){showToast('⚠️ الاسم والجنسية مطلوبان','#D9534F');return;}
  snapshot('إضافة عامل: '+name);
  const spIdx=sponsorIdxVal!==''&&sponsorIdxVal!=null?parseInt(sponsorIdxVal):-1;
  const wIdx=data.workers.length;
  data.workers.push({name,id,phone,nationality:nat,marketer,sponsorIdx:spIdx,
  transferDate:date,note,status:'active',addedDate:today()});
  if(spIdx>=0){
  const start=new Date(date);
  for(let m=0;m<12;m++){
  const d=new Date(start.getFullYear(),start.getMonth()+m,start.getDate());
  const ds=d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
  if(!data.transfers.find(t=>t.workerIdx===wIdx&&t.dueDate===ds))
  data.transfers.push({workerIdx:wIdx,sponsorIdx:spIdx,dueDate:ds,
  status:'pending',note:'',createdDate:today()});
  }
  }
  addOp('تم إضافة العامل: '+name);
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-worker'); showToast('✅ تم إضافة العامل: '+name);
  renderWorkers(); updateHeaderStats();
  populateSponsorSelects();
}
function delWorker(i){if(!confirm('حذف هذا العامل وكل حوالاته؟'))return;snapshot('حذف عامل');data.workers.splice(i,1);data.transfers=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.workerIdx!==i);saveData();renderWorkers();updateHeaderStats();}
function addSponsor(){
  name=document.getElementById('sp-name').value.trim();
  phone=document.getElementById('sp-phone').value.trim();
  id=document.getElementById('sp-id').value.trim();
  const iban=document.getElementById('sp-iban')?.value.trim()||'';
  note=document.getElementById('sp-note').value.trim();
  if(!name||!phone){showToast('⚠️ الاسم والجوال مطلوبان','#D9534F');return;}
  snapshot('إضافة كفيل: '+name);
  data.sponsors.push({name,phone,id,iban,note,addedDate:today()});
  addOp('تم إضافة الكفيل: '+name);
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-sponsor'); showToast('✅ تم إضافة الكفيل: '+name);
  renderSponsors();
}
function renderSponsors(filter=''){
  tbody=document.getElementById('sponsors-tbody');
  if(!tbody)return;
  const list=data.sponsors.filter(s=>!filter||s.name.includes(filter)||s.phone.includes(filter));
  if(!list.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" class="empty">لا يوجد كفلاء</td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML=list.map((s,ri)=>{
  const i=data.sponsors.indexOf(s);
  const myWorkers=data.workers.filter(w=>w.sponsorIdx===i);
  const pendingCnt=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.sponsorIdx===i&&t.status==='pending').length;
  const doneCnt=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.sponsorIdx===i&&t.status==='done').length;
  const workerNames=myWorkers.length
  ?myWorkers.slice(0,3).map(w=>'<span style="background:rgba(235,163,62,0.1);color:var(--accent-dk);font-size:10px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:12px;margin:1px">'+w.name+'</span>').join('')+(myWorkers.length>3?'<span style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:10px"> +'+( myWorkers.length-3)+'</span>':'')
  :'<span style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:11px">لا يوجد</span>';
  return '<tr>'
  +'<td>'+(ri+1)+'</td>'
  +'<td><strong>'+s.name+'</strong></td>'
  +'<td>📞 '+(s.phone||'—')+'</td>'
  +'<td><span style="font-weight:700;color:var(--ink)">'+myWorkers.length+'</span> عامل<br><div style="margin-top:4px">'+workerNames+'</div></td>'
  +'<td><span class="badge badge-teal">'+doneCnt+' مكتمل</span> <span class="badge badge-gold">'+pendingCnt+' معلق</span></td>'
  +'<td>'+(s.note||'—')+'</td>'
  +'<td style="display:flex;gap:4px">'
  +'<button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="openEditSponsor('+i+')" title="تعديل وعرض العمال">✏️ تعديل</button>'
  +'<button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delSponsor('+i+')" title="حذف">🗑️</button>'
  +'</td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}
function delSponsor(i){if(!confirm('حذف هذا الكفيل؟'))return;snapshot('حذف كفيل');data.sponsors.splice(i,1);saveData();renderSponsors();}
let trFilter='all';
function filterTransfers(f){trFilter=f;renderTransfers(f);}
function addTransfer(){
  wIdx=document.getElementById('tr-worker').value;
  date=document.getElementById('tr-date').value;
  amount=w?w.amount:1200;
  note=document.getElementById('tr-note').value.trim();
  if(wIdx===''||!date){showToast('⚠️ اختر العامل والتاريخ','#D9534F');return;}
  const w=data.workers[parseInt(wIdx)];
  snapshot('حوالة: '+w.name);
  data.transfers.push({workerIdx:parseInt(wIdx),sponsorIdx:w.sponsorIdx,amount,dueDate:date,status:'pending',note,createdDate:today()});
  addOp('حوالة: '+w.name+' — '+fmt(amount)+' ر.س');
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-transfer'); showToast('✅ تم تسجيل الحوالة');
  renderTransfers(trFilter);
}
function markTransferDone(i){
  if(data.transfers[i].status==='done')return;
  snapshot('تأكيد حوالة');
  data.transfers[i].status='done';
  w=data.workers[data.transfers[i].workerIdx];
  addOp('تم تأكيد حوالة: '+(w?w.name:''));
  saveData(); renderTransfers(trFilter); showToast('✅ تم تأكيد الحوالة');
}
function renderTransfers(f='all'){
  trFilter=f;
  const todayStr=today();
  const nextWeek=new Date(); nextWeek.setDate(nextWeek.getDate()+7);
  const weekStr=nextWeek.toISOString().split('T')[0];
  ['all','pending','done','today','week'].forEach(p=>{
  const b=document.getElementById('tpill-'+p);
  if(b) b.classList.toggle('active',p===f);
  });
  const search=(document.getElementById('trf-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  let rows=[...data.transfers];
  if(f==='pending') rows=rows.filter(t=>t.status!=='done');
  else if(f==='done') rows=rows.filter(t=>t.status==='done');
  else if(f==='today') rows=rows.filter(t=>t.dueDate===todayStr&&t.status!=='done');
  else if(f==='week')  rows=rows.filter(t=>t.dueDate>=todayStr&&t.dueDate<=weekStr&&t.status!=='done');
  if(search){
  rows=rows.filter(t=>{
  const w=data.workers[t.workerIdx];
  const sp=data.sponsors[t.sponsorIdx];
  return (w&&w.name.toLowerCase().includes(search))||
  (sp&&sp.name.toLowerCase().includes(search));
  });
  }
  rows=rows.sort((a,b)=>a.dueDate.localeCompare(b.dueDate));
  const all=data.transfers.length;
  const pending=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.status!=='done').length;
  const done=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.status==='done').length;
  const overdue=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.dueDate<todayStr&&t.status!=='done').length;
  const setEl=(id,v)=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.textContent=v;};
  setEl('trf-stat-all',all);setEl('trf-stat-pending',pending);
  setEl('trf-stat-done',done);setEl('trf-stat-overdue',overdue);
  const tbody=document.getElementById('tr-table-tbody');
  if(tbody){
  if(!rows.length){
  tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7"><div class="empty">لا توجد سجلات</div></td></tr>';
  } else{
  tbody.innerHTML=rows.map(t=>{
  const realIdx=data.transfers.indexOf(t);
  const w=data.workers[t.workerIdx];
  const sp=data.sponsors[t.sponsorIdx];
  const isOver=t.dueDate<todayStr&&t.status!=='done';
  const isToday=t.dueDate===todayStr&&t.status!=='done';
  const isDone=t.status==='done';
  const badgeColor=isDone?'#0d7a6a':isOver?'#D9534F':isToday?'#F9B264':'var(--ink3)';
  const badgeText=isDone?'✅ مكتمل':isOver?'⚠️ متأخر':isToday?'📅 اليوم':'⏳ معلق';
  return `<tr style="${isOver?'background:rgba(239,68,68,0.05)':''}${isToday?'background:rgba(235,163,62,0.07)':''}">
  <td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">${realIdx+1}</td>
  <td style="font-weight:700">${w?w.name:'—'}<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ink3)">${w?w.nationality||'':''}</span></td>
  <td>${sp?sp.name:'—'}<br><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ink3)">${sp?sp.phone||'':''}</span></td>
  <td style="font-weight:700;color:${isOver?'#D9534F':isToday?'#F9B264':'var(--ink)'}">${t.dueDate}</td>
  <td><span style="padding:3px 8px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;background:${badgeColor}22;color:${badgeColor}">${badgeText}</span></td>
  <td style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink3)">${t.note||'—'}</td>
  <td>
  <div style="display:flex;gap:4px;flex-wrap:wrap">
  ${!isDone?`<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="font-size:10px" onclick="markTransferDone(${realIdx});renderTransfers(trFilter)">✅</button>`:''}
  <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="font-size:10px" onclick="openEditTransfer(${realIdx})">✏️</button>
  <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" style="font-size:10px" onclick="delTransfer(${realIdx})">🗑️</button>
  </div>
  </td>
  </tr>`;
  }).join('');
  }
  }
  renderTransfersBySponsor(rows);
}
function renderTransfersBySponsor(rows){
  const container=document.getElementById('tr-sponsor-groups');
  if(!container) return;
  const todayStr=today();
  const groups={};
  rows.forEach(t=>{
  const spIdx=t.sponsorIdx;
  const key=spIdx>=0?spIdx:'none';
  if(!groups[key]) groups[key]=[];
  groups[key].push(t);
  });
  if(!Object.keys(groups).length){
  container.innerHTML='<div class="empty" style="padding:40px;text-align:center">لا توجد سجلات</div>';
  return;
  }
  container.innerHTML=Object.entries(groups).map(([key,trList])=>{
  const sp=key!=='none'?data.sponsors[parseInt(key)]:null;
  const doneCount=trList.filter(t=>t.status==='done').length;
  const overdueCount=trList.filter(t=>t.dueDate<todayStr&&t.status!=='done').length;
  return `<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;margin-bottom:14px;overflow:hidden">
  <div style="padding:14px 16px;background:var(--surface);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;cursor:pointer" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.style.display=this.nextElementSibling.style.display==='none'?'block':'none'">
  <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px">
  <div style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:10px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),#8b5cf6);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:900;color:#092650">${sp?sp.name.charAt(0):'?'}</div>
  <div>
  <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:14px">${sp?sp.name:'بدون كفيل'}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">${sp?sp.phone||'':''}</div>
  </div>
  </div>
  <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">
  <span style="padding:3px 10px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line)">${trList.length} موعد</span>
  ${overdueCount?`<span style="padding:3px 10px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;background:rgba(239,68,68,0.15);color:var(--red)">⚠️ ${overdueCount} متأخر</span>`:''}
  ${doneCount?`<span style="padding:3px 10px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;background:rgba(45,212,191,0.15);color:#0d7a6a">✅ ${doneCount} مكتمل</span>`:''}
  ${sp?`<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="font-size:10px" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openEditSponsor(${parseInt(key)})">✏️ تعديل الكفيل</button>`:''}
  </div>
  </div>
  <div style="padding:12px 16px">
  <table style="width:100%;font-size:12px"><thead><tr style="color:var(--ink3)">
  <th style="text-align:right;padding:4px 6px">العامل</th>
  <th style="text-align:right;padding:4px 6px">الاستحقاق</th>
  <th style="text-align:right;padding:4px 6px">الحالة</th>
  <th style="text-align:right;padding:4px 6px">إجراءات</th>
  </tr></thead><tbody>
  ${trList.map(t=>{
  const realIdx=data.transfers.indexOf(t);
  const w=data.workers[t.workerIdx];
  const isOver=t.dueDate<todayStr&&t.status!=='done';
  const isToday=t.dueDate===todayStr&&t.status!=='done';
  const isDone=t.status==='done';
  const c=isDone?'#0d7a6a':isOver?'#D9534F':isToday?'#F9B264':'var(--ink3)';
  const label=isDone?'✅ مكتمل':isOver?'⚠️ متأخر':isToday?'📅 اليوم':'⏳ معلق';
  return `<tr style="border-top:1px solid var(--line)">
  <td style="padding:6px"><a href="#" onclick="event.preventDefault();openEditWorker(${t.workerIdx})" style="color:var(--ink);font-weight:700">${w?w.name:'—'}</a></td>
  <td style="padding:6px;font-weight:700;color:${isOver?'#D9534F':isToday?'#F9B264':'inherit'}">${t.dueDate}</td>
  <td style="padding:6px"><span style="color:${c};font-weight:700">${label}</span></td>
  <td style="padding:6px"><div style="display:flex;gap:4px">
  ${!isDone?`<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="font-size:10px" onclick="markTransferDone(${realIdx});renderTransfers(trFilter)">✅</button>`:''}
  <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="font-size:10px" onclick="openEditTransfer(${realIdx})">✏️</button>
  <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" style="font-size:10px" onclick="delTransfer(${realIdx})">🗑️</button>
  </div></td>
  </tr>`;
  }).join('')}
  </tbody></table>
  </div>
  </div>`;
  }).join('');
}
function setTrView(v){
  document.getElementById('tr-view-table').style.display=v==='table'?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('tr-view-sponsor').style.display=v==='sponsor'?'block':'none';
  document.getElementById('tview-table').style.opacity=v==='table'?'1':'0.5';
  document.getElementById('tview-sponsor').style.opacity=v==='sponsor'?'1':'0.5';
  trCurrentView=v;
}
let trCurrentView='table';
function delTransfer(i){if(!confirm('حذف هذه الحوالة؟'))return;snapshot('حذف حوالة');data.transfers.splice(i,1);saveData();renderTransfers(trFilter);}
let calYear=new Date().getFullYear(), calMonth=new Date().getMonth();
const MONTHS_AR=['يناير','فبراير','مارس','أبريل','مايو','يونيو','يوليو','أغسطس','سبتمبر','أكتوبر','نوفمبر','ديسمبر'];
function prevCal(){calMonth--;if(calMonth<0){calMonth=11;calYear--;}renderCal();}
function nextCal(){calMonth++;if(calMonth>11){calMonth=0;calYear++;}renderCal();}
function renderCal(){
  const lbl=document.getElementById('cal-label');if(lbl)lbl.textContent=MONTHS_AR[calMonth]+' '+calYear;
  grid=document.getElementById('cal-grid');if(!grid)return;
  todayStr=today(); const daysInMonth=new Date(calYear,calMonth+1,0).getDate(); const firstDay=new Date(calYear,calMonth,1).getDay();
  grid.innerHTML='';
  for(let i=0;i<firstDay;i++){const b=document.createElement('div');b.className='cal-day';grid.appendChild(b);}
  for(let d=1;d<=daysInMonth;d++){
  ds=calYear+'-'+String(calMonth+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d).padStart(2,'0');
  const trs=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.dueDate===ds);
  el=document.createElement('div');
  el.className='cal-day'+(trs.length?' has-event':'')+(ds===todayStr?' today':'');
  el.innerHTML='<span>'+d+'</span>'+(trs.length?'<div class="cal-dot"></div>':'');
  if(trs.length)el.onclick=()=>showCalDay(ds,trs);
  grid.appendChild(el);
  }
  const ms=calYear+'-'+String(calMonth+1).padStart(2,'0');
  const mTrs=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.dueDate.startsWith(ms)).sort((a,b)=>a.dueDate.localeCompare(b.dueDate));
  list=document.getElementById('cal-list');
  if(!list)return;
  if(!mTrs.length){list.innerHTML='<div class="empty">لا توجد حوالات هذا الشهر</div>';return;}
  list.innerHTML=mTrs.map(t=>{const w=data.workers[t.workerIdx];const sp=data.sponsors[t.sponsorIdx];const i=data.transfers.indexOf(t);return '<div style="background:var(--surface);border-radius:8px;padding:10px;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between"><div><div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700">'+(w?w.name:'—')+'</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">'+(sp?sp.name:'—')+' | '+t.dueDate+'</div></div><div style="text-align:left"><div style="font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-dk)">'+fmt(t.amount)+' ر.س</div>'+(t.status!=='done'?'<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="markTransferDone('+i+');renderCal()">✅</button>':'<span class="badge badge-teal">مكتمل</span>')+'</div></div>';}).join('');
}
function showCalDay(ds,trs){const list=document.getElementById('cal-list');if(!list)return;list.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-dk);margin-bottom:10px">📅 '+ds+'</div>'+trs.map(t=>{const w=data.workers[t.workerIdx];const sp=data.sponsors[t.sponsorIdx];const i=data.transfers.indexOf(t);return '<div style="background:var(--surface);border-radius:8px;padding:10px;margin-bottom:8px"><div style="font-weight:700">'+(w?w.name:'—')+'</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">'+(sp?sp.name:'—')+(sp&&sp.iban?'<br>🏦 '+sp.iban:'')+'</div><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:6px"><span style="color:var(--accent-dk);font-weight:700">'+fmt(t.amount)+' ر.س</span>'+(t.status!=='done'?'<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="markTransferDone('+i+');renderCal()">✅ تأكيد</button>':'<span class="badge badge-teal">مكتمل</span>')+'</div></div>';}).join('');}
function renderSaudi(filter=''){
  rows=data.saudiEmployees.filter(e=>!filter||e.name.includes(filter)||e.nid.includes(filter));
  now=new Date();
  const alerts=rows.filter(e=>{if(!e.excludeDate)return false;const d=new Date(e.excludeDate);return(d-now)/(1000*3600*24)<=30;}).length;
  const expired=rows.filter(e=>{if(!e.excludeDate)return false;return new Date(e.excludeDate)<now;}).length;
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('sau-total',data.saudiEmployees.length);
  set('sau-pct',data.workers.length?Math.round(data.saudiEmployees.length/(data.workers.length+data.saudiEmployees.length)*100)+'%':'0%');
  set('sau-alerts',alerts); set('sau-expired',expired);
  tbody=document.getElementById('saudi-tbody');
  if(!tbody)return;
  if(!rows.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty" style="color:var(--ink3)">لا يوجد موظفون</td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML=rows.map((e,ri)=>{
  i=data.saudiEmployees.indexOf(e);
  const now2=new Date(); let badge='';
  if(e.excludeDate){const d=new Date(e.excludeDate);const days=Math.round((d-now2)/(1000*3600*24));
  if(days<0)badge='<span class="s-badge-red">منتهية</span>';
  else if(days<=30)badge='<span class="s-badge-orange">⚠️ خلال '+days+' يوم</span>';
  else badge='<span class="s-badge-green">✅ نشط</span>';}
  else badge='<span class="s-badge-green">✅ نشط</span>';
  return '<tr style="color:#1a2236;border-bottom:1px solid #f1f5f9"><td>'+(ri+1)+'</td><td><strong>'+e.name+'</strong></td><td>'+e.nid+'</td><td>'+(e.title||'—')+'</td><td>'+(e.hireDate||'—')+'</td><td>'+fmt(e.salary)+' ر.س</td><td>'+(e.excludeDate||'—')+'</td><td>'+badge+'</td><td style="display:flex;gap:4px"><button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="owfe(this)" data-t="saudi" data-i="'+i+'" title="تعديل">✏️</button><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delSaudi('+i+')">🗑️</button></td></tr>';
  }).join('');
}
function delSaudi(i){if(!confirm('حذف؟'))return;snapshot('حذف موظف');data.saudiEmployees.splice(i,1);saveData();renderSaudi();}
function checkSaudiAlerts(){const now=new Date();return(data.saudiEmployees||[]).filter(e=>{if(!e.excludeDate)return false;const d=new Date(e.excludeDate);return(d-now)/(1000*3600*24)<=30;}).length;}
function addContact(){
  name=document.getElementById('con-name').value.trim();
  phone=document.getElementById('con-phone').value.trim();
  const type=document.getElementById('con-type').value;
  const city=document.getElementById('con-city').value.trim();
  const email=document.getElementById('con-email')?.value.trim();
  id=document.getElementById('con-id').value.trim();
  note=document.getElementById('con-note').value.trim();
  if(!name||!phone){showToast('⚠️ الاسم والجوال مطلوبان','#D9534F');return;}
  if(!data.customers)data.customers=[];
  data.customers.push({name,phone,type,city,email,id,note,addedDate:today()});
  addOp('جهة اتصال: '+name);
  saveData(); closeModal('m-add-contact'); showToast('✅ تم الإضافة: '+name);
  renderContacts();
}
function delContact(i){if(!confirm('حذف جهة الاتصال؟'))return;data.customers.splice(i,1);saveData();renderContacts();}
function exportCSV(type){
  rows=[];let name='تصدير';
  if(type==='contacts'){name='قاعدة_العملاء';rows=[['النوع','الاسم','الجوال','الهوية','ملاحظة']];data.workers.forEach(w=>rows.push(['عامل',w.name,w.phone,w.id,'']));data.sponsors.forEach(s=>rows.push(['كفيل',s.name,s.phone,s.id||'','']));(data.customers||[]).forEach(c=>rows.push([c.type,c.name,c.phone,c.id||'',c.note||'']));}
  else if(type==='sales'){name='المبيعات';rows=[['التاريخ','الخدمة','الكمية','السعر','الإجمالي','الربح','طريقة الدفع','العميل']];data.sales.forEach(r=>rows.push([r.date,r.name,r.qty,r.price,r.qty*r.price,r.qty*(r.price-r.cost),r.payMethod||'',r.client||'']));}
  else if(type==='expenses'){name='النفقات';rows=[['التاريخ','النفقة','الفئة','المبلغ','طريقة الدفع','المورد']];data.expensesList.forEach(r=>rows.push([r.date,r.name,r.cat,r.amount,r.payMethod||'',r.vendor||'']));}
  else if(type==='inventory'){name='الجرد';rows=[['رمز SKU','الاسم','الفئة','الكمية','سعر الشراء','سعر البيع','القيمة']];data.inventory.forEach(r=>rows.push([r.sku||'',r.name,r.cat,r.qty,r.buy,r.sell,r.qty*r.buy]));}
  else if(type==='accounting'){name='المحاسبة';rows=[['التاريخ','النوع','الوصف','المبلغ','طريقة الدفع','الفئة']];data.accounting.forEach(r=>rows.push([r.date,r.type==='income'?'إيراد':'مصروف',r.desc,r.amount,r.payMethod||'',r.cat||'']));}
  csv=rows.map(r=>r.join(',')).join('\n');
  a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob(['\ufeff'+csv],{type:'text/csv'}));a.download=name+'.csv';a.click();
}
function initAtt(){const d=document.getElementById('att-date');if(d&&!d.value)d.value=today();const m=document.getElementById('att-report-month');if(m&&!m.value)m.value=today().slice(0,7);const h=document.getElementById('att-hist-month');if(h&&!h.value)h.value=today().slice(0,7);const lbl=document.getElementById('att-date-label');if(lbl)lbl.textContent='اليوم: '+new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-SA',{weekday:'long',year:'numeric',month:'long',day:'numeric'});}
function renderAttendance(){
  if(!data.attendance)data.attendance=[];
  date=document.getElementById('att-date')?.value||today();
  search=(document.getElementById('att-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  workers=data.workers.filter(w=>w.status==='active'&&(!search||w.name.toLowerCase().includes(search)));
  const recs=data.attendance.filter(a=>a.date===date);
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('att-present',recs.filter(a=>a.status==='present').length);
  set('att-absent',recs.filter(a=>a.status==='absent').length);
  set('att-late',recs.filter(a=>a.status==='late').length);
  set('att-total',data.workers.filter(w=>w.status==='active').length);
  list=document.getElementById('att-list');if(!list)return;
  if(!workers.length){list.innerHTML='<div class="empty">لا يوجد عمال نشطون</div>';return;}
  list.innerHTML=workers.map(w=>{
  i=data.workers.indexOf(w);
  const rec=data.attendance.find(a=>a.workerIdx===i&&a.date===date);
  status=rec?rec.status:'none';
  const labels={present:'✅ حاضر',absent:'❌ غائب',late:'⏰ متأخر',none:'— لم يسجل'};
  const colors={present:'#0d7a6a',absent:'#D9534F',late:'#F9B264',none:'var(--ink3)'};
  return '<div class="att-row"><div><div class="att-name">'+w.name+'</div><div class="att-meta">📞 '+w.phone+'</div></div><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><input type="time" id="att-t-'+i+'" value="'+(rec?.time||'')+'" class="form-control" style="width:110px;padding:7px 10px"><button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="setAtt('+i+',\'present\')">✅</button><button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="setAtt('+i+',\'late\')">⏰</button><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="setAtt('+i+',\'absent\')">❌</button><span style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:'+colors[status]+'">'+labels[status]+'</span></div></div>';
  }).join('');
}
function setAtt(wi,status){
  if(!data.attendance)data.attendance=[];
  date=document.getElementById('att-date')?.value||today();
  const time=document.getElementById('att-t-'+wi)?.value||'';
  const ex=data.attendance.findIndex(a=>a.workerIdx===wi&&a.date===date);
  rec={workerIdx:wi,date,status,time,addedDate:today()};
  if(ex>=0)data.attendance[ex]=rec;else data.attendance.push(rec);
  saveData(); renderAttendance();
}
function markAllPresent(){
  if(!data.attendance)data.attendance=[];
  date=document.getElementById('att-date')?.value||today();
  data.workers.filter(w=>w.status==='active').forEach(w=>{const i=data.workers.indexOf(w);const ex=data.attendance.findIndex(a=>a.workerIdx===i&&a.date===date);const rec={workerIdx:i,date,status:'present',time:'',addedDate:today()};if(ex>=0)data.attendance[ex]=rec;else data.attendance.push(rec);});
  saveData();renderAttendance();showToast('✅ تم تحضير جميع العمال');
}
function renderAttReport(){
  const month=document.getElementById('att-report-month')?.value||today().slice(0,7);
  recs=data.attendance.filter(a=>a.date.startsWith(month));
  tbody=document.getElementById('att-rpt-tbody');if(!tbody)return;
  workers=data.workers.filter(w=>w.status==='active');
  if(!workers.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" class="empty">لا يوجد عمال</td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML=workers.map((w,ri)=>{const i=data.workers.indexOf(w);const wr=recs.filter(a=>a.workerIdx===i);const p=wr.filter(a=>a.status==='present').length;const ab=wr.filter(a=>a.status==='absent').length;const lt=wr.filter(a=>a.status==='late').length;const tot=p+ab+lt;const pct=tot?Math.round((p+lt)/tot*100):0;return '<tr><td>'+(ri+1)+'</td><td><strong>'+w.name+'</strong></td><td style="color:#0d7a6a">'+p+'</td><td style="color:var(--red)">'+ab+'</td><td style="color:var(--accent-dk)">'+lt+'</td><td><span class="badge '+(pct>=80?'badge-teal':pct>=60?'badge-gold':'badge-red')+'">'+pct+'%</span></td></tr>';}).join('');
}
function renderAttHistory(){
  if(!data.attendance)data.attendance=[];
  search=(document.getElementById('att-hist-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  month=document.getElementById('att-hist-month')?.value||'';
  recs=[...data.attendance].sort((a,b)=>b.date.localeCompare(a.date));
  if(month)recs=recs.filter(a=>a.date.startsWith(month));
  if(search)recs=recs.filter(a=>{const w=data.workers[a.workerIdx];return w&&w.name.toLowerCase().includes(search);});
  tbody=document.getElementById('att-hist-tbody');if(!tbody)return;
  if(!recs.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="6" class="empty">لا يوجد سجلات</td></tr>';return;}
  const ls={present:'<span class="badge badge-teal">✅ حاضر</span>',absent:'<span class="badge badge-red">❌ غائب</span>',late:'<span class="badge badge-gold">⏰ متأخر</span>'};
  tbody.innerHTML=recs.map((a,ri)=>{const w=data.workers[a.workerIdx];const i=data.attendance.indexOf(a);return '<tr><td>'+(ri+1)+'</td><td>'+a.date+'</td><td>'+(w?w.name:'—')+'</td><td>'+(ls[a.status]||a.status)+'</td><td>'+(a.time||'—')+'</td><td><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delAttRec('+i+')">🗑️</button></td></tr>';}).join('');
}
function delAttRec(i){data.attendance.splice(i,1);saveData();renderAttHistory();}
function handleDrop(e){e.preventDefault();e.currentTarget.style.borderColor='var(--line)';const dt=e.dataTransfer;if(dt.files.length){const inp={files:dt.files,value:''};handleFileUpload(inp);}}
function renderFiles(){
  search=(document.getElementById('file-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  cat=(typeof currentFileFilter!=='undefined'?currentFileFilter:'all')||'all';
  let files=(data.files||[]).filter(f=>(!search||f.name.toLowerCase().includes(search))&&(!cat||f.cat===cat));
  grid=document.getElementById('files-container');if(!grid)return;
  if(!files.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="empty" style="grid-column:1/-1">لا توجد ملفات</div>';return;}
  const icons={pdf:'📄',image:'🖼️',excel:'📊',word:'📝',other:'📎'};
  const getType=f=>f.type.includes('pdf')?'pdf':f.type.includes('image')?'image':f.type.includes('sheet')||f.type.includes('excel')?'excel':f.type.includes('word')?'word':'other';
  grid.innerHTML=files.map(f=>{const i=(data.files||[]).indexOf(f);const t=getType(f);return '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;padding:16px;transition:all 0.2s" onmouseover="this.style.borderColor=\'var(--accent)\'" onmouseout="this.style.borderColor=\'var(--line)\'"><div style="font-size:32px;margin-bottom:8px">'+icons[t]+'</div><div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:4px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">'+f.name+'</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3);margin-bottom:10px">'+f.uploadDate+'</div><div style="display:flex;gap:6px"><a href="'+f.data+'" download="'+f.name+'" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="text-decoration:none;flex:1;justify-content:center">⬇ تحميل</a><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="delFile('+i+')">🗑️</button></div></div>';}).join('');
}
function delFile(i){if(!confirm('حذف هذا الملف؟'))return;data.files.splice(i,1);saveData();renderFiles();}
function initApp(){
  const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('hulul_theme');
  if(savedTheme) document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', savedTheme);
  if(savedTheme==='light'){ const icon=document.getElementById('theme-icon'); if(icon) icon.className='fa-solid fa-sun'; }
  document.getElementById('app').style.display='flex';
  loadData();
  if(!data.workers)            data.workers=[];
  if(!data.sponsors)           data.sponsors=[];
  if(!data.transfers)          data.transfers=[];
  if(!data.accounting)         data.accounting=[];
  if(!data.sales)              data.sales=[];
  if(!data.expensesList)       data.expensesList=[];
  if(!data.inventory)          data.inventory=[];
  if(!data.saudiEmployees)     data.saudiEmployees=[];
  if(!data.customers)          data.customers=[];
  if(!data.attendance)         data.attendance=[];
  if(!data.files)              data.files=[];
  if(!data.ops)                data.ops=[];
  if(!data.debts)              data.debts=[];
  if(!data.loyalty)            data.loyalty=[];
  if(!data.tasks)              data.tasks=[];
  if(!data.receipts)           data.receipts=[];
  if(!data.categories)         data.categories=['مشتريات','مبيعات','رواتب','فواتير','إيجار','مرافق','نقل','صيانة','تأمين','أخرى'];
  if(!data.vendors)            data.vendors=[];
  if(!data.brokers)            data.brokers=[];
  if(!data.brokerEntitlements) data.brokerEntitlements=[];
  if(!data.agents)           data.agents=[];
  if(!data.agentRequests)    data.agentRequests=[];
  if(!data.importHistory)    data.importHistory=[];
  if(!data.serviceRequests)  data.serviceRequests=[];
  if(!data._seeded_v1){ data._seeded_v1=true; addOp('تهيئة النظام'); }
  showSec('dashboard');
  updateBrokerAlertBadge();
  if(typeof updateDashboardTaskWidget==='function') updateDashboardTaskWidget();
  saveData();
  setTimeout(checkTransferAlerts,200);
  document.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]').forEach(i=>{if(!i.value)i.value=today();});
  document.querySelectorAll('input[type="month"]').forEach(i=>{if(!i.value)i.value=today().slice(0,7);});
  startAutoBackup();
  updateBackupStatusBar();
  updateDashDate();
  setInterval(updateFooterClock, 1000);
  updateFooterClock();
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initApp);

const SECTIONS_NEW = ['dashboard','workers','transfers','accounting','sales','expenses','inventory','saudi','contacts','loyalty','attendance','files'];
function showSec(id){
  document.querySelectorAll('.section').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('active'));
  const el = document.getElementById('sec-'+id);
  const nav = document.getElementById('nav-'+id);
  if(el) el.classList.add('active');
  if(nav) nav.classList.add('active');
  currentSection = id;
  const titles ={
  dashboard:'لوحة التحكم', workers:'إدارة العمال والكفلاء', transfers:'الحوالات',
  accounting:'المحاسبة المتكاملة', sales:'الديون والمبيعات', expenses:'المصروفات',
  inventory:'الجرد', saudi:'متابعة العمال الشهرية', contacts:'المراسلات وجهات الاتصال',
  loyalty:'نظام الولاء', attendance:'الحضور والانصراف', files:'رفع الملفات',
  tasks:'المهام اليومية', receipts:'التقارير المالية', brokers:'مستحقات الوسطاء',
  services:'دليل الخدمات', ops:'سجل النشاطات',
  import:'استيراد وتحليل الملفات', agents:'المتابعون وطلبات الخدمة',
  hulul:'حلول — الموظف الرقمي الذكي'
  };
  const titleEl = document.getElementById('topbar-title');
  if(titleEl) titleEl.textContent = titles[id] || id;
  if(id==='dashboard'){ setTimeout(checkTransferAlerts,50); renderOverview(); updateDashboardAlerts(); updateDashKPIs(); renderDashTasks(); renderDashOps(); updateDashDate(); }
  if(id==='workers'){ renderWorkers(); renderSponsors(); updateWorkerKPIs(); }
  if(id==='transfers')  renderTransfers(trFilter||'all');
  if(id==='accounting'){ renderAccounting(); updateAccKPIs(); }
  if(id==='sales'){ renderSales(); updateSalesKPIs(); }
  if(id==='expenses'){ renderExpenses(); updateExpKPIs(); }
  if(id==='inventory'){ renderInventory(); updateInvKPIs(); }
  if(id==='saudi'){ renderSaudi(); checkSaudiAlerts(); }
  if(id==='contacts')   renderContacts();
  if(id==='loyalty')    renderLoyalty();
  if(id==='attendance'){ initAtt(); renderAttendance(); }
  if(id==='files'){ renderFiles(); updateFileStats(); }
  if(id==='tasks')      renderTasks();
  if(id==='receipts'){ renderReceipts(); updateReceiptKPIs(); }
  if(id==='brokers'){ renderBrokers(); renderBrokerEntitlements(); }
  if(id==='reports'){ renderReports(); }
  if(id==='gemini'){ updateGeminiKeyStatus(); }
  if(id==='hulul'){ initHulul(); }
  if(id==='import'){ renderImportHistory(); populateAgentSelect('imp-agent-assign'); }
  if(id==='agents'){ renderAgents(); renderAgentDebtReport(); populateAgentSelect('imp-agent-assign'); }
  if(id==='services')   renderServices();
  if(id==='ops')        renderOps();
  updateHeaderStats();
  updateBrokerAlertBadge();
  if(window.innerWidth<=768) closeSidebar();
}
function toggleSidebar(){
  const sb=document.getElementById('sidebar');
  const main=document.getElementById('main');
  if(window.innerWidth<=768){
    sb.classList.toggle('open');
  } else {
    sb.classList.toggle('collapsed');
    main.classList.toggle('expanded');
    localStorage.setItem('sb_state', sb.classList.contains('collapsed')?'1':'0');
  }
}
function closeSidebar(){
  const sb=document.getElementById('sidebar');
  sb?.classList.remove('mobile-open');
}
activeConFilter='all';
function renderWorkers(filter='', natFilter=''){
  if(!data.workers) data.workers=[];
  list = data.workers;
  if(filter) list = list.filter(w=>w.name.toLowerCase().includes(filter.toLowerCase())||w.id.includes(filter));
  if(natFilter) list = list.filter(w=>w.nationality===natFilter);
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('w-total', data.workers.length);
  set('w-sponsors', (data.sponsors||[]).length);
  const monthlyTotal = data.workers.reduce((s,w)=>s+(w.salary||0),0);
  const commTotal    = data.workers.reduce((s,w)=>s+(w.commission||0),0);
  set('w-monthly', fmt(monthlyTotal)+' ر.س');
  set('w-commissions', fmt(commTotal)+' ر.س');
  tbody = document.getElementById('workers-tbody');
  if(!tbody) return;
  if(!list.length){tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="10"><div class="empty"><div class="empty-icon">👷</div>لا يوجد عمال</div></td></tr>';return;}
  tbody.innerHTML = list.map((w,i)=>{
  sp = (data.sponsors||[])[w.sponsorIdx];
  return `<tr>
  <td class="td-muted">${i+1}</td>
  <td><strong>${w.name}</strong></td>
  <td>${w.nationality||'-'}</td>
  <td class="td-muted" style="font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace">${w.id||'-'}</td>
  <td class="td-muted" style="font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace">${w.phone||'-'}</td>
  <td>${sp?sp.name:'<span class="td-muted">—</span>'}</td>
  <td><span class="badge badge-gold">${fmt(w.salary||0)} ر.س</span></td>
  <td class="td-muted">${w.marketer||'—'}</td>
  <td class="td-muted">${w.date||'-'}</td>
  <td><button class="btn btn-red btn-xs" onclick="delWorker(${data.workers.indexOf(w)})">🗑</button></td>
  </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}
function updateInvKPIs(){
  const inv = data.inventory||[];
  total = inv.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.qty*i.buy,0);
  profit = inv.reduce((s,i)=>s+i.qty*(i.sell-i.buy),0);
  const low = inv.filter(i=>i.qty<=(i.min||0)).length;
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('inv-count',  inv.length);
  set('inv-value',  fmt(total)+' ر.س');
  set('inv-low',    low);
  set('inv-profit', fmt(profit)+' ر.س');
}
function renderContacts(){
  if(!data.customers) data.customers=[];
  search=(document.getElementById('contacts-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  TYPE_INFO={
  worker:{avatar:'👷',color:'#F9B264',  label:'عامل',  badge:'badge-gold'},
  sponsor:{avatar:'🤝',color:'#0d7a6a',  label:'كفيل',  badge:'badge-teal'},
  office:{avatar:'🏢',color:'#3A7CA5',  label:'مكتب',  badge:'badge-blue'},
  customer:{avatar:'🧑',color:'#2E7D64', label:'زبون',  badge:'badge-green'},
  supplier:{avatar:'🏭',color:'#6d28d9',label:'مورد',  badge:'badge-purple'},
  other:{avatar:'📋',color:'var(--ink3)', label:'أخرى',  badge:'badge-gray'}
  };
  contacts=[];
  if(activeConFilter==='all'||activeConFilter==='workers')
  data.workers.forEach((w,i)=>contacts.push({...TYPE_INFO.worker,name:w.name,phone:w.phone,id:w.id,sub:(data.sponsors[w.sponsorIdx]?.name||'')+(w.nationality?' — '+w.nationality:''),srcType:'worker',srcIdx:i}));
  if(activeConFilter==='all'||activeConFilter==='sponsors')
  data.sponsors.forEach((s,i)=>contacts.push({...TYPE_INFO.sponsor,name:s.name,phone:s.phone,id:s.id||'',sub:'كفيل — '+data.workers.filter(w=>w.sponsorIdx===i).length+' عامل',srcType:'sponsor',srcIdx:i}));
  if(activeConFilter==='all'||activeConFilter==='offices')
  data.customers.filter(c=>c.type==='office').forEach(c=>{const i=data.customers.indexOf(c);contacts.push({...TYPE_INFO.office,name:c.name,phone:c.phone,id:c.id||'',sub:'🏢 مكتب'+(c.city?' — '+c.city:''),srcType:'contact',srcIdx:i});});
  if(activeConFilter==='all'||activeConFilter==='customers')
  data.customers.filter(c=>c.type!=='office').forEach(c=>{const i=data.customers.indexOf(c);const inf=TYPE_INFO[c.type]||TYPE_INFO.other;contacts.push({...inf,name:c.name,phone:c.phone,id:c.id||'',sub:inf.label+(c.city?' — '+c.city:''),srcType:'contact',srcIdx:i});});
  if(search) contacts=contacts.filter(c=>c.name.toLowerCase().includes(search)||c.phone.includes(search));
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('con-cnt-workers',  data.workers.length);
  set('con-cnt-sponsors', data.sponsors.length);
  set('con-cnt-offices',  data.customers.filter(c=>c.type==='office').length);
  set('con-cnt-customers',data.customers.filter(c=>c.type!=='office').length);
  grid=document.getElementById('contacts-grid');
  if(!grid) return;
  if(!contacts.length){grid.innerHTML='<div class="empty" style="grid-column:1/-1"><div class="empty-icon">📞</div>لا توجد جهات اتصال</div>';return;}
  grid.innerHTML=contacts.map(c=>`
  <div class="con-card">
  <div class="con-stripe" style="background:${c.color}"></div>
  <div class="con-body">
  <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:8px">
  <div class="con-avatar" style="background:${c.color.replace(')',',0.15)').replace('var(','rgba(')}">{c.avatar}</div>
  <div>
  <div class="con-name">${c.name}</div>
  <span class="badge ${c.badge}">${c.label}</span>
  </div>
  </div>
  <div class="con-phone">📞 ${c.phone}</div>
  ${c.id?`<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3);font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',monospace">🪪 ${c.id}</div>`:''}
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3);margin-top:2px">${c.sub}</div>
  <div class="con-actions">
  <a href="tel:${c.phone}" class="btn btn-primary btn-xs" style="text-decoration:none;flex:1;justify-content:center">📞 اتصال</a>
  <a href="https://wa.me/966${c.phone.replace(/^0/,'')}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-outline btn-xs" style="text-decoration:none;flex:1;justify-content:center">💬</a>
  ${c.srcType==='contact'?`<button class="btn btn-red btn-xs" onclick="delContact(${c.srcIdx})">🗑</button>`:''}
  </div>
  </div>
  </div>
  `).join('');
}
function updateHeaderStats(){
  set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('h-workers',   (data.workers||[]).length);
  const allInc=(data.accounting||[]).filter(e=>e.type==='income').reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0)
  +(data.sales||[]).reduce((s,r)=>s+r.qty*r.price,0);
  allExp=(data.accounting||[]).filter(e=>e.type==='expense').reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0)
  +(data.expensesList||[]).reduce((s,e)=>s+e.amount,0);
  set('h-profit', fmt(allInc-allExp));
  todayStr=today();
  set('h-transfers', (data.transfers||[]).filter(t=>t.status!=='done'&&t.dueDate&&t.dueDate<=todayStr).length);
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.classList.contains('modal-overlay'))e.target.classList.remove('open');});
function confirmDel(msg){return confirm(msg||'هل تريد الحذف؟');}
function openEditWorker(idx){
  const w=data.workers[idx];
  if(!w) return;
  document.getElementById('ew-idx').value=idx;
  document.getElementById('ew-name').value=w.name||'';
  document.getElementById('ew-id').value=w.id||'';
  document.getElementById('ew-phone').value=w.phone||'';
  document.getElementById('ew-note').value=w.note||'';
  document.getElementById('ew-date').value=w.transferDate||today();
  document.getElementById('ew-marketer').value=w.marketer||'';
  const natSel=document.getElementById('ew-nat');
  if(natSel){
  [...natSel.options].forEach(o=>{o.selected=(o.value===w.nationality||o.text===w.nationality);});
  if(!natSel.value&&w.nationality){
  const opt=document.createElement('option');
  opt.value=opt.text=w.nationality; natSel.appendChild(opt); natSel.value=w.nationality;
  }
  }
  const spSel=document.getElementById('ew-sponsor');
  if(spSel){
  spSel.innerHTML='<option value="">بدون كفيل</option>';
  data.sponsors.forEach((s,i)=>{
  const o=document.createElement('option');
  o.value=i; o.text=s.name; if(i===w.sponsorIdx) o.selected=true;
  spSel.appendChild(o);
  });
  }
  openModal('m-edit-worker');
}
function saveEditWorker(){
  const idx=parseInt(document.getElementById('ew-idx').value);
  const w=data.workers[idx];
  if(!w) return;
  const name=document.getElementById('ew-name').value.trim();
  if(!name){showToast('⚠️ الاسم مطلوب','#D9534F');return;}
  snapshot('تعديل عامل: '+w.name);
  const oldSpIdx=w.sponsorIdx;
  const newSpIdx=document.getElementById('ew-sponsor').value!==''?parseInt(document.getElementById('ew-sponsor').value):-1;
  w.name=name;
  w.id=document.getElementById('ew-id').value.trim();
  w.phone=document.getElementById('ew-phone').value.trim();
  w.nationality=document.getElementById('ew-nat').value;
  w.marketer=document.getElementById('ew-marketer').value.trim();
  w.transferDate=document.getElementById('ew-date').value||today();
  w.note=document.getElementById('ew-note').value.trim();
  if(newSpIdx!==oldSpIdx){
  w.sponsorIdx=newSpIdx;
  data.transfers.filter(t=>t.workerIdx===idx).forEach(t=>{t.sponsorIdx=newSpIdx;});
  }
  addOp('تم تعديل العامل: '+name);
  saveData(); closeModal('m-edit-worker'); showToast('✅ تم تعديل بيانات العامل');
  renderWorkers(); renderTransfers(trFilter);
}
function openEditSponsor(idx){
  const sp=data.sponsors[idx];
  if(!sp) return;
  document.getElementById('es-id').value=idx;
  document.getElementById('es-name').value=sp.name||'';
  document.getElementById('es-phone').value=sp.phone||'';
  document.getElementById('es-id').value=sp.id||'';
  document.getElementById('es-note').value=sp.note||'';
  const workersList=document.getElementById('es-workers-list');
  if(workersList){
  const myWorkers=data.workers.map((w,i)=>({...w,_i:i})).filter(w=>w.sponsorIdx===idx);
  if(!myWorkers.length){
  workersList.innerHTML='<div style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:12px;padding:6px">لا يوجد عمال تحت هذه الكفالة</div>';
  } else{
  workersList.innerHTML=myWorkers.map(w=>`
  <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;background:var(--bg);margin-bottom:6px">
  <div>
  <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px">${w.name}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">${w.nationality||''} ${w.phone?'· '+w.phone:''}</div>
  </div>
  <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="closeModal('m-edit-sponsor');openEditWorker(${w._i})" style="font-size:11px">✏️ تعديل</button>
  </div>`).join('');
  }
  }
  openModal('m-edit-sponsor');
}

}

}
function saveEditTransfer(){
  const idx=parseInt(document.getElementById('etr-id').value);
  const t=data.transfers[idx];
  if(!t) return;
  snapshot('تعديل موعد استحقاق');
  t.dueDate=document.getElementById('etr-date').value||t.dueDate;
  t.status=document.getElementById('etr-status').value;
  t.note=document.getElementById('etr-note').value.trim();
  addOp('تم تعديل موعد استحقاق');
  saveData(); closeModal('m-edit-transfer'); showToast('✅ تم تعديل الموعد');
  renderTransfers(trFilter);
}
function checkTransferAlerts(){
  const todayStr=today();
  const nextWeek=new Date(); nextWeek.setDate(nextWeek.getDate()+7);
  const weekStr=nextWeek.toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const overdue=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.dueDate<todayStr&&t.status!=='done');
  const todayDue=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.dueDate===todayStr&&t.status!=='done');
  const upcoming=data.transfers.filter(t=>t.dueDate>todayStr&&t.dueDate<=weekStr&&t.status!=='done');
  const alertBox=document.getElementById('tr-alert-box');
  if(!alertBox) return;
  if(!overdue.length&&!todayDue.length&&!upcoming.length){
  alertBox.style.display='none'; return;
  }
  let html='';
  if(overdue.length){
  const names=overdue.slice(0,3).map(t=>{
  const w=data.workers[t.workerIdx];
  const sp=data.sponsors[t.sponsorIdx];
  return `<b>${w?w.name:'—'}</b> → ${sp?sp.name:'—'} (${t.dueDate})`;
  }).join('<br>');
  html+=`<div style="margin-bottom:10px">
  <div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--red);margin-bottom:4px">⚠️ ${overdue.length} موعد متأخر</div>
  <div style="font-size:12px;line-height:1.8">${names}${overdue.length>3?`<br>و ${overdue.length-3} أخرى...`:''}</div>
  </div>`;
  }
  if(todayDue.length){
  const names=todayDue.slice(0,3).map(t=>{
  const w=data.workers[t.workerIdx];
  const sp=data.sponsors[t.sponsorIdx];
  return `<b>${w?w.name:'—'}</b> → ${sp?sp.name:'—'}`;
  }).join('<br>');
  html+=`<div style="margin-bottom:10px">
  <div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--accent-dk);margin-bottom:4px">📅 ${todayDue.length} موعد اليوم</div>
  <div style="font-size:12px;line-height:1.8">${names}${todayDue.length>3?`<br>و ${todayDue.length-3} أخرى...`:''}</div>
  </div>`;
  }
  if(upcoming.length){
  html+=`<div>
  <div style="font-weight:800;color:#0d7a6a;margin-bottom:4px">📆 ${upcoming.length} موعد خلال هذا الأسبوع</div>
  </div>`;
  }
  html+=`<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="margin-top:8px;font-size:11px" onclick="showSec('transfers')">📋 عرض جدول الاستحقاق</button>`;
  alertBox.innerHTML=html;
  alertBox.style.display='block';
}
function populateSponsorSelects(){
  ['wk-sponsor','ew-sponsor'].forEach(id=>{
  const sel=document.getElementById(id);
  if(!sel) return;
  const cur=sel.value;
  sel.innerHTML='<option value="">بدون كفيل</option>';
  data.sponsors.forEach((s,i)=>{
  const o=document.createElement('option');
  o.value=i; o.text=s.name; sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  if(cur) sel.value=cur;
  });
}
function updateDashboardAlerts(){
  const box = document.getElementById('tr-alert-box');
  if (!box) return;
  const todayStr = today();
  const soon = new Date(); soon.setDate(soon.getDate() + 7);
  const soonStr = soon.toISOString().split('T')[0];
