  const alerts = (data.transfers||[]).filter(t => t.status === 'pending' && t.dueDate <= soonStr);
  if (!alerts.length){ box.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  const overdue  = alerts.filter(t => t.dueDate < todayStr);
  const dueToday = alerts.filter(t => t.dueDate === todayStr);
  const upcoming = alerts.filter(t => t.dueDate > todayStr);
  box.style.display = 'block';
  const items = alerts.map(t =>{
  const w = (data.workers||[])[t.workerIdx];
  const sp = t.sponsorIdx >= 0 ? (data.sponsors||[])[t.sponsorIdx] : null;
  const isOver = t.dueDate < todayStr;
  const isToday = t.dueDate === todayStr;
  const clr = isOver ? '#ef4444' : isToday ? 'var(--accent2)' : '#3b82f6';
  const lbl = isOver ? '🔴 متأخر' : isToday ? '🟡 اليوم' : '🔵 قريب';
  const ri = data.transfers.indexOf(t);
  return '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid rgba('+(isOver?'239,68,68':isToday?'245,158,11':'59,130,246')+',0.2);border-radius:9px;padding:9px 13px;margin-bottom:6px">'
  +'<div><span style="font-weight:800;color:var(--ink)">'+(w?w.name:'؟')+'</span>'
  +'<span style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:12px;margin:0 8px">←</span>'
  +'<span style="color:'+clr+';font-size:12px">'+(sp?sp.name:'')+'</span></div>'
  +'<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">'
  +'<span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">'+t.dueDate+'</span>'
  +'<span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:'+clr+'">'+lbl+'</span>'
  +'<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="height:24px;font-size:10px;padding:0 8px" onclick="openEditTransfer('+ri+')">تعديل</button>'
  +'</div></div>';
  }).join('');
  box.innerHTML = '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:var(--ink);margin-bottom:10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">'
  +'<span style="font-size:18px">🔔</span> تنبيهات جدول الاستحقاق '
  +(overdue.length?'<span style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.15);color:#ef4444;font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px">'+overdue.length+' متأخر</span>':'')
  +(dueToday.length?'<span style="background:rgba(245,158,11,0.15);color:var(--accent2);font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px">'+dueToday.length+' اليوم</span>':'')
  +(upcoming.length?'<span style="background:rgba(59,130,246,0.15);color:#3b82f6;font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px">'+upcoming.length+' قريب</span>':'')
  +'</div>'+items;
}
let pendingImport = null; // holds parsed data waiting for confirmation
function handleFileUpload(inp){
  Array.from(inp.files).forEach(f =>{
  const ext = f.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase();
  if (['xlsx','xls','csv'].includes(ext)){
  analyzeExcelFile(f);
  } else if (['docx','doc'].includes(ext)){
  analyzeWordFile(f);
  } else{
  const r = new FileReader();
  r.onload = e =>{
  if (!data.files) data.files = [];
  data.files.push({name:f.name, type:f.type, size:f.size,
  data:e.target.result, cat:'أخرى', uploadDate:today()});
  saveData(); renderFiles();
  };
  r.readAsDataURL(f);
  }
  });
  inp.value = '';
}
function analyzeExcelFile(file){
  openAnalysisModal('📊 تحليل ملف Excel: ' + file.name);
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e =>{
  try{
  if (typeof XLSX === 'undefined'){
  setFaStatus('⚠️ مكتبة Excel غير محملة بعد، يرجى الانتظار أو إعادة المحاولة.', 'warn');
  return;
  }
  const wb = XLSX.read(new Uint8Array(e.target.result),{type:'array'});
  const result = parseExcelWorkbook(wb, file.name);
  displayAnalysisResult(result, file);
  } catch(err){
  setFaStatus('❌ خطأ في قراءة الملف: ' + err.message, 'error');
  }
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
}
function parseExcelWorkbook(wb, fileName){
  const result ={
  fileName, fileType: 'excel',
  sheets: [],
  extracted:{ workers:[], sales:[], expenses:[], inventory:[], contacts:[], accounting:[] },
  warnings: [],
  stats:{}
  };
  wb.SheetNames.forEach(shName =>{
  const ws = wb.Sheets[shName];
  const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws,{header:1, defval:''});
  if (!rows.length) return;
  const headers = (rows[0]||[]).map(h => String(h||'').trim());
  const dataRows = rows.slice(1).filter(r => r.some(c => c !== ''));
  const sheet ={name: shName, headers, rowCount: dataRows.length, rows: dataRows};
  result.sheets.push(sheet);
  headers.forEach((h, ci) =>{
  const vals = dataRows.map(r => parseFloat(r[ci])).filter(v => !isNaN(v));
  if (vals.length > 1){
  const sum = vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b, 0);
  result.stats[shName+'_'+h] ={
  sum: Math.round(sum*100)/100,
  avg: Math.round(sum/vals.length*100)/100,
  min: Math.min(...vals),
  max: Math.max(...vals),
  count: vals.length
  };
  }
  });
  classifyExcelSheet(sheet, result);
  });
  return result;
}
function classifyExcelSheet(sheet, result){
  const headersLower = sheet.headers.map(h => h.toLowerCase());
  const headersAr = sheet.headers.join('');
  const isWorker = headersAr.includes('عامل') || headersAr.includes('موظف') ||
  headersAr.includes('جنسية') || headersAr.includes('كفيل') ||
  headersLower.some(h => ['name','worker','employee','nationality'].includes(h));
  const isSales = headersAr.includes('مبيعة') || headersAr.includes('مبيع') ||
  headersAr.includes('عميل') || headersAr.includes('سعر') ||
  headersLower.some(h => ['sale','sales','price','client','revenue'].includes(h));
  const isExpense = headersAr.includes('مصروف') || headersAr.includes('نفقة') ||
  headersAr.includes('تكلفة') || headersAr.includes('دفع') ||
  headersLower.some(h => ['expense','cost','payment'].includes(h));
  const isInventory = headersAr.includes('مخزون') || headersAr.includes('صنف') ||
  headersAr.includes('كمية') || headersAr.includes('منتج') ||
  headersLower.some(h => ['inventory','stock','qty','quantity','product','item'].includes(h));
  const isContact = headersAr.includes('جوال') || headersAr.includes('هاتف') ||
  headersAr.includes('عنوان') || headersAr.includes('بريد') ||
  headersLower.some(h => ['phone','mobile','email','address','contact'].includes(h));
  sheet.rows.forEach(row =>{
  if (isWorker){
  const w = extractWorkerRow(sheet.headers, row);
  if (w) result.extracted.workers.push(w);
  }
  if (isSales){
  const s = extractSaleRow(sheet.headers, row);
  if (s) result.extracted.sales.push(s);
  }
  if (isExpense){
  const ex = extractExpenseRow(sheet.headers, row);
  if (ex) result.extracted.expenses.push(ex);
  }
  if (isInventory){
  const inv = extractInventoryRow(sheet.headers, row);
  if (inv) result.extracted.inventory.push(inv);
  }
  if (isContact){
  const con = extractContactRow(sheet.headers, row);
  if (con) result.extracted.contacts.push(con);
  }
  if (!isWorker && !isSales && !isExpense && !isInventory && !isContact){
  const nums = row.filter(c => !isNaN(parseFloat(c)) && c !== '');
  if (nums.length >= 2){
  result.warnings.push('صف غير مصنّف: ' + row.slice(0,3).filter(c=>c!=='').join(' | '));
  }
  }
  });
}
function findCol(headers, keys){
  const hl = headers.map(h=>String(h).toLowerCase());
  for (const k of keys){
  const i = hl.findIndex(h => h.includes(k.toLowerCase()) || headers[hl.indexOf(h)]?.includes(k));
  if (i >= 0) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}
function extractWorkerRow(headers, row){
  const nameI  = findCol(headers, ['اسم','name','الاسم','employee','موظف','عامل']);
  const idI    = findCol(headers, ['هوية','id','رقم الهوية','iqama','اقامة']);
  const phoneI = findCol(headers, ['جوال','phone','هاتف','tel','mobile']);
  const natI   = findCol(headers, ['جنسية','nationality','nat']);
  const noteI  = findCol(headers, ['ملاحظة','note','notes','ملاحظات']);
  const name = nameI >= 0 ? String(row[nameI]||'').trim() : '';
  if (!name || name.length < 2) return null;
  return{
  name,
  id:          idI>=0    ? String(row[idI]||'').trim()    : '',
  phone:       phoneI>=0 ? String(row[phoneI]||'').trim() : '',
  nationality: natI>=0   ? String(row[natI]||'').trim()   : '',
  note:        noteI>=0  ? String(row[noteI]||'').trim()  : '',
  sponsorIdx: -1, status:'active', addedDate:today()
  };
}
function extractSaleRow(headers, row){
  const nameI  = findCol(headers, ['اسم','name','صنف','منتج','بضاعة','product','item']);
  const qtyI   = findCol(headers, ['كمية','qty','quantity','عدد']);
  const priceI = findCol(headers, ['سعر','price','مبلغ','amount','قيمة']);
  const dateI  = findCol(headers, ['تاريخ','date']);
  const name = nameI>=0 ? String(row[nameI]||'').trim() : '';
  const price = priceI>=0 ? parseFloat(row[priceI])||0 : 0;
  if (!name || price === 0) return null;
  return{
  name, qty: qtyI>=0 ? parseInt(row[qtyI])||1 : 1,
  price, cat:'عام', method:'نقد',
  date: dateI>=0 ? String(row[dateI]||today()) : today(),
  addedDate:today()
  };
}
function extractExpenseRow(headers, row){
  const nameI   = findCol(headers, ['اسم','name','وصف','بيان','مصروف','نفقة','description']);
  const amtI    = findCol(headers, ['مبلغ','amount','قيمة','تكلفة','cost','price','سعر']);
  const dateI   = findCol(headers, ['تاريخ','date']);
  const catI    = findCol(headers, ['فئة','cat','category','نوع','type']);
  const name = nameI>=0 ? String(row[nameI]||'').trim() : '';
  const amount = amtI>=0 ? parseFloat(row[amtI])||0 : 0;
  if (!name || amount === 0) return null;
  return{
  name, amount, cat: catI>=0 ? String(row[catI]||'عام') : 'عام',
  method:'نقد', date: dateI>=0 ? String(row[dateI]||today()) : today(),
  addedDate:today()
  };
}
function extractInventoryRow(headers, row){
  const nameI = findCol(headers, ['اسم','name','صنف','منتج','بضاعة','product','item']);
  const qtyI  = findCol(headers, ['كمية','qty','quantity','عدد','مخزون']);
  const buyI  = findCol(headers, ['شراء','buy','تكلفة','cost','سعر الشراء']);
  const sellI = findCol(headers, ['بيع','sell','سعر البيع','price']);
  const name = nameI>=0 ? String(row[nameI]||'').trim() : '';
  const qty  = qtyI>=0 ? parseInt(row[qtyI])||0 : 0;
  if (!name || qty === 0) return null;
  return{
  name, qty, cat:'عام', sku:'',
  buy: buyI>=0 ? parseFloat(row[buyI])||0 : 0,
  sell: sellI>=0 ? parseFloat(row[sellI])||0 : 0,
  minQty:1, addedDate:today()
  };
}
function extractContactRow(headers, row){
  const nameI  = findCol(headers, ['اسم','name','الاسم']);
  const phoneI = findCol(headers, ['جوال','phone','هاتف','tel','mobile']);
  const typeI  = findCol(headers, ['نوع','type','صفة']);
  const noteI  = findCol(headers, ['ملاحظة','note','notes']);
  const name  = nameI>=0  ? String(row[nameI]||'').trim()  : '';
  const phone = phoneI>=0 ? String(row[phoneI]||'').trim() : '';
  if (!name && !phone) return null;
  return{
  name: name||phone, phone,
  type: typeI>=0 ? String(row[typeI]||'عميل') : 'عميل',
  note: noteI>=0 ? String(row[noteI]||'') : '',
  addedDate:today()
  };
}
function analyzeWordFile(file){
  openAnalysisModal('📄 تحليل ملف Word: ' + file.name);
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e =>{
  try{
  if (typeof mammoth === 'undefined'){
  setFaStatus('⚠️ مكتبة Word غير محملة بعد، يرجى الانتظار أو إعادة المحاولة.', 'warn');
  return;
  }
  mammoth.extractRawText({arrayBuffer: e.target.result}).then(res =>{
  const result = parseWordText(res.value, file.name);
  displayAnalysisResult(result, file);
  }).catch(err =>{
  setFaStatus('❌ خطأ في قراءة ملف Word: ' + err.message, 'error');
  });
  } catch(err){
  setFaStatus('❌ خطأ: ' + err.message, 'error');
  }
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
}
function parseWordText(text, fileName){
  const result ={
  fileName, fileType:'word',
  fullText: text,
  sections: [],
  extracted:{ workers:[], notes:[] },
  warnings: [],
  keyPoints: []
  };
  const lines = text.split('\n').map(l=>l.trim()).filter(l=>l.length>1);
  lines.forEach(line =>{
  const workerMatch = line.match(/(?:عامل|موظف|كفيل)?[:\s]*([^\d]{3,30})[,،\s]+(?:هوية|id|رقم)?[:\s]*(\d{8,12})?/i);
  if (workerMatch && workerMatch[1]?.trim().length >= 3){
  result.keyPoints.push({ type:'worker', text:line, name:workerMatch[1].trim(), id:workerMatch[2]||'' });
  } else if (line.length > 15 && line.length < 200){
  result.keyPoints.push({ type:'text', text:line });
  }
  });
  lines.forEach(line =>{
  const w = tryParseWorkerLine(line);
  if (w) result.extracted.workers.push(w);
  });
  result.sections.push({
  title:'محتوى الملف',
  points: result.keyPoints.slice(0,30).map(p=>p.text)
  });
  if (result.extracted.workers.length === 0 && result.keyPoints.length === 0){
  result.warnings.push('لم يتم العثور على بيانات قابلة للاستيراد في هذا الملف');
  }
  return result;
}
function tryParseWorkerLine(line){
  const parts = line.split(/[|،,\t]+/);
  if (parts.length < 2) return null;
  let name='', id='', phone='', nat='';
  parts.forEach(p =>{
  const kv = p.split(/[:：]/);
  if (kv.length < 2) return;
  const key=kv[0].trim().toLowerCase(), val=kv[1].trim();
  if (['اسم','name','الاسم','عامل','موظف'].some(k=>key.includes(k))) name=val;
  else if (['هوية','id','رقم'].some(k=>key.includes(k))) id=val;
  else if (['جوال','phone','هاتف'].some(k=>key.includes(k))) phone=val;
  else if (['جنسية','nat'].some(k=>key.includes(k))) nat=val;
  });
  if (!name || name.length < 2) return null;
  return{name, id, phone, nationality:nat, note:'', sponsorIdx:-1, status:'active', addedDate:today()};
}
function openAnalysisModal(title){
  document.getElementById('fa-title').textContent = title;
  document.getElementById('fa-status').textContent = '⏳ جاري تحليل الملف...';
  document.getElementById('fa-status').style.color = '#F9B264';
  document.getElementById('fa-summary').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('fa-details').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('fa-actions').style.display = 'none';
  openModal('m-file-analysis');
}
function setFaStatus(msg, type='info'){
  const el = document.getElementById('fa-status');
  if (!el) return;
  el.textContent = msg;
  el.style.color = type==='error'?'#ef4444':type==='warn'?'var(--accent2)':type==='ok'?'#10b981':'var(--ink3)';
}
function displayAnalysisResult(result, file){
  pendingImport ={ result, file };
  const totals ={
  workers:   result.extracted?.workers?.length   || 0,
  sales:     result.extracted?.sales?.length     || 0,
  expenses:  result.extracted?.expenses?.length  || 0,
  inventory: result.extracted?.inventory?.length || 0,
  contacts:  result.extracted?.contacts?.length  || 0,
  sheets:    result.sheets?.length               || 0,
  keyPoints: result.keyPoints?.length            || 0,
  };
  const total = totals.workers + totals.sales + totals.expenses + totals.inventory + totals.contacts;
  setFaStatus(total > 0 ? `✅ تم استخراج ${total} سجل قابل للاستيراد` : '⚠️ لم يتم العثور على بيانات قابلة للاستيراد', total>0?'ok':'warn');
  const summaryEl = document.getElementById('fa-summary');
  const cards = [
  totals.sheets     ?{icon:'📄', val:totals.sheets,    lbl:'ورقة Excel'} : null,
  totals.keyPoints  ?{icon:'📝', val:totals.keyPoints, lbl:'نقطة رئيسية'} : null,
  totals.workers    ?{icon:'👷', val:totals.workers,    lbl:'عامل'} : null,
  totals.sales      ?{icon:'💰', val:totals.sales,      lbl:'مبيعة'} : null,
  totals.expenses   ?{icon:'💸', val:totals.expenses,   lbl:'نفقة'} : null,
  totals.inventory  ?{icon:'📦', val:totals.inventory,  lbl:'صنف'} : null,
  totals.contacts   ?{icon:'👤', val:totals.contacts,   lbl:'جهة اتصال'} : null,
  ].filter(Boolean);
  document.getElementById('fa-summary-cards').innerHTML = cards.map(c=>
  `<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;text-align:center">
  <div style="font-size:22px">${c.icon}</div>
  <div style="font-size:20px;font-weight:900;color:var(--accent-dk)">${c.val}</div>
  <div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">${c.lbl}</div>
  </div>`).join('') || '<div style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:13px">لا توجد بيانات مصنّفة</div>';
  const secAdded = [];
  if (totals.workers>0)   secAdded.push('👷 العمال ('+totals.workers+')');
  if (totals.sales>0)     secAdded.push('💰 المبيعات ('+totals.sales+')');
  if (totals.expenses>0)  secAdded.push('💸 المصروفات ('+totals.expenses+')');
  if (totals.inventory>0) secAdded.push('📦 المخزون ('+totals.inventory+')');
  if (totals.contacts>0)  secAdded.push('👤 جهات الاتصال ('+totals.contacts+')');
  const secBox = document.getElementById('fa-sections-added');
  const secList = document.getElementById('fa-sections-list');
  if (secAdded.length){
  secBox.style.display = 'block';
  secList.innerHTML = secAdded.map(s=>`<div style="padding:3px 0">• ${s}</div>`).join('');
  } else{
  secBox.style.display = 'none';
  }
  const warns = result.warnings || [];
  const warnBox = document.getElementById('fa-warnings-box');
  const warnList = document.getElementById('fa-warnings-list');
  if (warns.length){
  warnBox.style.display = 'block';
  warnList.innerHTML = warns.slice(0,10).map(w=>`<div style="padding:2px 0">⚠️ ${w}</div>`).join('');
  } else{
  warnBox.style.display = 'none';
  }
  summaryEl.style.display = 'block';
  buildDetailTabs(result);
  const actionsEl = document.getElementById('fa-actions');
  if (actionsEl) actionsEl.style.display = total > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
}
function buildDetailTabs(result){
  const tabsEl = document.getElementById('fa-tabs');
  const contentEl = document.getElementById('fa-tab-content');
  const detailsEl = document.getElementById('fa-details');
  const tabs = [];
  if (result.fileType === 'excel' && result.sheets?.length){
  result.sheets.forEach(sh => tabs.push({id:'sheet_'+sh.name, label:'📄 '+sh.name, content: buildSheetPreview(sh, result.stats)}));
  }
  if (result.extracted?.workers?.length)   tabs.push({id:'workers',   label:'👷 عمال',         content: buildWorkerPreview(result.extracted.workers)});
  if (result.extracted?.sales?.length)     tabs.push({id:'sales',     label:'💰 مبيعات',       content: buildSimplePreview(result.extracted.sales, ['name','qty','price','date'])});
  if (result.extracted?.expenses?.length)  tabs.push({id:'expenses',  label:'💸 نفقات',        content: buildSimplePreview(result.extracted.expenses, ['name','amount','cat','date'])});
  if (result.extracted?.inventory?.length) tabs.push({id:'inventory', label:'📦 مخزون',        content: buildSimplePreview(result.extracted.inventory, ['name','qty','buy','sell'])});
  if (result.extracted?.contacts?.length)  tabs.push({id:'contacts',  label:'👤 اتصالات',      content: buildSimplePreview(result.extracted.contacts, ['name','phone','type'])});
  if (result.fileType === 'word' && result.sections?.length) tabs.push({id:'text', label:'📝 المحتوى', content: buildWordPreview(result)});
  if (!tabs.length){ detailsEl.style.display='none'; return; }
  detailsEl.style.display = 'block';
  tabsEl.innerHTML = tabs.map((t,i)=>
  `<button class="btn ${i===0?'btn-gold':'btn-outline'} btn-sm" onclick="switchFaTab('${t.id}',this)">${t.label}</button>`
  ).join('');
  window._faTabs ={};
  tabs.forEach(t => window._faTabs[t.id] = t.content);
  contentEl.innerHTML = tabs[0]?.content || '';
}
function switchFaTab(id, btn){
  document.querySelectorAll('#fa-tabs button').forEach(b => b.className = b.className.replace('btn-gold','btn-outline'));
  btn.className = btn.className.replace('btn-outline','btn-gold');
  document.getElementById('fa-tab-content').innerHTML = window._faTabs?.[id] || '';
}
function buildSheetPreview(sh, stats){
  if (!sh.rows.length) return '<div style="color:var(--ink3)">لا توجد بيانات</div>';
  const statKeys = Object.keys(stats).filter(k=>k.startsWith(sh.name+'_'));
  let statsHtml = '';
  if (statKeys.length){
  statsHtml = '<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px">'+
  statKeys.map(k=>{
  const s=stats[k]; const col=k.replace(sh.name+'_','');
  return `<div style="background:rgba(235,163,62,0.08);border:1px solid rgba(235,163,62,0.2);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 12px;font-size:11px">
  <div style="font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-dk)">${col}</div>
  <div>المجموع: ${s.sum.toLocaleString('ar-SA')}</div>
  <div>المتوسط: ${s.avg.toLocaleString('ar-SA')}</div>
  <div>الأعلى: ${s.max} | الأدنى: ${s.min}</div>
  </div>`;
  }).join('')+'</div>';
  }
  const maxRows = 20;
  const tableRows = sh.rows.slice(0, maxRows).map(r=>
  '<tr>'+sh.headers.map((_,i)=>`<td style="padding:5px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">${r[i]??''}</td>`).join('')+'</tr>'
  ).join('');
  return statsHtml +
  `<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:100%">
  <thead><tr>${sh.headers.map(h=>`<th style="padding:6px 8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px;font-weight:700;text-align:right">${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
  <tbody>${tableRows}</tbody>
  </table></div>
  ${sh.rows.length>maxRows?`<div style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:11px;margin-top:6px">... و${sh.rows.length-maxRows} صف إضافي</div>`:''}`;
}
function buildWorkerPreview(workers){
  return `<table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:100%">
  <thead><tr>
  <th style="padding:6px 8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">الاسم</th>
  <th style="padding:6px 8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">الهوية</th>
  <th style="padding:6px 8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">الجوال</th>
  <th style="padding:6px 8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">الجنسية</th>
  <th style="padding:6px 8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">الحالة</th>
  </tr></thead>
  <tbody>${workers.map(w=>{
  const existing = data.workers.find(ew=>ew.name===w.name||(w.id&&ew.id===w.id));
  const badge = existing
  ? '<span style="color:var(--accent2);font-size:10px;font-weight:700">🔄 تحديث</span>'
  : '<span style="color:#10b981;font-size:10px;font-weight:700">➕ جديد</span>';
  return `<tr>
  <td style="padding:5px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px;font-weight:700">${w.name}</td>
  <td style="padding:5px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">${w.id||'—'}</td>
  <td style="padding:5px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">${w.phone||'—'}</td>
  <td style="padding:5px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">${w.nationality||'—'}</td>
  <td style="padding:5px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">${badge}</td>
  </tr>`;
  }).join('')}</tbody>
  </table>`;
}
function buildSimplePreview(items, keys){
  if (!items.length) return '<div style="color:var(--ink3)">لا توجد بيانات</div>';
  const labelMap ={name:'الاسم',qty:'الكمية',price:'السعر',date:'التاريخ',
  amount:'المبلغ',cat:'الفئة',buy:'سعر الشراء',sell:'سعر البيع',
  phone:'الجوال',type:'النوع',method:'طريقة الدفع'};
  return `<table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:100%">
  <thead><tr>${keys.map(k=>`<th style="padding:6px 8px;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">${labelMap[k]||k}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
  <tbody>${items.slice(0,20).map(item=>
  '<tr>'+keys.map(k=>`<td style="padding:5px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line);font-size:11px">${item[k]??'—'}</td>`).join('')+'</tr>'
  ).join('')}</tbody>
  </table>${items.length>20?`<div style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:11px;margin-top:6px">... و${items.length-20} سجل إضافي</div>`:''}`;
}
function buildWordPreview(result){
  const pts = result.keyPoints||[];
  return `<div style="background:var(--surface);border-radius:10px;padding:14px;max-height:300px;overflow-y:auto">
  ${pts.slice(0,40).map(p=>`<div style="padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);font-size:12px;color:var(--ink)">${p.type==='worker'?'👷 ':'📝 '}${p.text}</div>`).join('')}
  ${pts.length>40?`<div style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:11px;padding-top:6px">... و${pts.length-40} نقطة إضافية</div>`:''}
  </div>`;
}
function confirmFileImport(){
  if (!pendingImport) return;
  const{result, file} = pendingImport;
  const ext = (result.extracted ||{});
  let summary = [];
  snapshot('استيراد ملف: ' + result.fileName);
  (ext.workers||[]).forEach(w =>{
  const existing = data.workers.findIndex(ew => ew.name===w.name || (w.id && ew.id===w.id));
  if (existing >= 0){
  const ew = data.workers[existing];
  if (w.id)          ew.id          = w.id;
  if (w.phone)       ew.phone       = w.phone;
  if (w.nationality) ew.nationality = w.nationality;
  if (w.note)        ew.note        = w.note;
  } else{
  if (!data.workers) data.workers = [];
  data.workers.push(w);
  }
  });
  if (ext.workers?.length) summary.push('👷 ' + ext.workers.length + ' عامل');
  (ext.sales||[]).forEach(s =>{
  if (!data.sales) data.sales = [];
  data.sales.push(s);
  });
  if (ext.sales?.length) summary.push('💰 ' + ext.sales.length + ' مبيعة');
  (ext.expenses||[]).forEach(e =>{
  if (!data.expensesList) data.expensesList = [];
  data.expensesList.push(e);
  });
  if (ext.expenses?.length) summary.push('💸 ' + ext.expenses.length + ' نفقة');
  (ext.inventory||[]).forEach(item =>{
  if (!data.inventory) data.inventory = [];
  const existing = data.inventory.findIndex(i => i.name === item.name);
  if (existing >= 0){
  data.inventory[existing].qty += item.qty;
  } else{
  data.inventory.push(item);
  }
  });
  if (ext.inventory?.length) summary.push('📦 ' + ext.inventory.length + ' صنف');
  (ext.contacts||[]).forEach(c =>{
  if (!data.customers) data.customers = [];
  const existing = data.customers.findIndex(ec => ec.phone && ec.phone===c.phone);
  if (existing < 0) data.customers.push(c);
  });
  if (ext.contacts?.length) summary.push('👤 ' + ext.contacts.length + ' جهة اتصال');
  if (!data.files) data.files = [];
  data.files.push({
  name: file.name, type: file.type||'', size: file.size||0,
  data:'', cat: 'مستورد', uploadDate: today(),
  importSummary: summary.join(' | ')
  });
  saveData();
  addOp('تم استيراد: ' + result.fileName + ' — ' + (summary.join('، ')||'لا بيانات'));
  closeModal('m-file-analysis');
  pendingImport = null;
  if (ext.workers?.length){ renderWorkers(); updateHeaderStats(); }
  if (ext.sales?.length)     renderSales();
  if (ext.expenses?.length)  renderExpenses();
  if (ext.inventory?.length) renderInventory();
  if (ext.contacts?.length)  renderContacts();
  renderFiles();
  showToast('✅ تم الاستيراد: ' + (summary.join(' | ') || 'لا بيانات قابلة للاستيراد'));
}
function openWorkerFullEdit(type, idx){
  const item = type==='saudi' ? (data.saudiEmployees||[])[idx] : (data.workers||[])[idx];
  if (!item) return;
  document.getElementById('wfe-type').value = type;
  document.getElementById('wfe-idx').value  = idx;
  const formEl = document.getElementById('wfe-form-body');
  if (type === 'worker'){
  const spOpts = '<option value="-1">بدون كفيل</option>' +
  (data.sponsors||[]).map((s,i)=>`<option value="${i}" ${i===item.sponsorIdx?'selected':''}>${s.name}</option>`).join('');
  formEl.innerHTML = `
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>الاسم *</label><input id="wfe-name2" value="${item.name||''}"></div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>رقم الهوية</label><input id="wfe-id2" value="${item.id||''}"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>الجوال</label><input id="wfe-phone2" value="${item.phone||''}"></div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>الجنسية</label>
  <select id="wfe-nat2">
  <option value="">اختر...</option>
  ${['يمني','إثيوبي','مصري','سوداني','باكستاني','هندي','بنغلاديشي','أخرى'].map(n=>`<option ${item.nationality===n?'selected':''}>${n}</option>`).join('')}
  </select>
  </div>
  </div>
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>الكفيل</label><select id="wfe-sponsor2">${spOpts}</select></div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>تاريخ الانضمام</label><input id="wfe-date2" type="date" value="${item.transferDate||item.addedDate||''}"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>الحالة</label>
  <select id="wfe-status2">
  <option value="active" ${item.status==='active'?'selected':''}>نشط</option>
  <option value="inactive" ${item.status==='inactive'?'selected':''}>غير نشط</option>
  <option value="transferred" ${item.status==='transferred'?'selected':''}>محوّل</option>
  </select>
  </div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>ملاحظة</label><input id="wfe-note2" value="${item.note||''}"></div>
  </div>`;
  } else{
  formEl.innerHTML = `
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>الاسم *</label><input id="wfe2-name" value="${item.name||''}"></div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>الرقم الوطني</label><input id="wfe-nid" value="${item.nid||item.id||''}"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>المسمى الوظيفي</label><input id="wfe-title2" value="${item.title||''}"></div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>الراتب</label><input id="wfe-salary2" type="number" value="${item.salary||''}"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>تاريخ التوظيف</label><input id="wfe-hire2" type="date" value="${item.hireDate||''}"></div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>تاريخ الاستبعاد</label><input id="wfe-exclude2" type="date" value="${item.excludeDate||''}"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="form-grid">
  <div class="form-group"><label>القسم</label><input id="wfe-dept2" value="${item.department||''}"></div>
  <div class="form-group"><label>ملاحظة</label><input id="wfe2-note" value="${item.note||''}"></div>
  </div>`;
  }
  openModal('m-worker-full-edit');
}
function saveWorkerFullEdit(){
  const type = document.getElementById('wfe-type').value;
  const idx  = parseInt(document.getElementById('wfe-idx').value);
  const name = document.getElementById('wfe-name')?.value.trim();
  if (!name){ showToast('⚠️ الاسم مطلوب','#D9534F'); return; }
  if (type === 'worker'){
  const w = data.workers[idx];
  if (!w) return;
  snapshot('تعديل عامل: ' + name);
  w.name        = name;
  w.id          = document.getElementById('wfe-id')?.value.trim()||'';
  w.phone       = document.getElementById('wfe-phone')?.value.trim()||'';
  w.nationality = document.getElementById('wfe-nat')?.value||'';
  w.sponsorIdx  = parseInt(document.getElementById('wfe-sponsor')?.value||'-1');
  w.transferDate= document.getElementById('wfe-date')?.value||'';
  w.status      = document.getElementById('wfe-status')?.value||'active';
  w.note        = document.getElementById('wfe-note')?.value.trim()||'';
  data.transfers.forEach(t=>{ if(t.workerIdx===idx) t.sponsorIdx=w.sponsorIdx; });
  saveData(); renderWorkers(); updateHeaderStats();
  } else{
  const e = data.saudiEmployees[idx];
  if (!e) return;
  snapshot('تعديل موظف سعودي: ' + name);
  e.name        = name;
  e.nid         = document.getElementById('wfe-nid')?.value.trim()||'';
  e.title       = document.getElementById('wfe-title')?.value.trim()||'';
  e.salary      = parseFloat(document.getElementById('wfe-salary')?.value)||0;
  e.hireDate    = document.getElementById('wfe-hire')?.value||'';
  e.excludeDate = document.getElementById('wfe-exclude')?.value||'';
  e.department  = document.getElementById('wfe-dept')?.value.trim()||'';
  e.note        = document.getElementById('wfe-note')?.value.trim()||'';
  saveData(); renderSaudi();
  }
  addOp('تم تعديل بيانات: ' + name);
  closeModal('m-worker-full-edit');
  showToast('✅ تم حفظ التعديلات');
}
function owfe(btn){openWorkerFullEdit(btn.dataset.t,parseInt(btn.dataset.i));}
if (!data.tasks) data.tasks = [];
let currentTaskFilter = 'all';
function openTaskForm(editId){
  const modal = document.getElementById('m-add-task');
  const title = document.getElementById('task-modal-title');
  if (editId !== undefined){
  const t = data.tasks.find(x => x.id === editId);
  if (!t) return;
  title.textContent = '✏️ تعديل المهمة';
  document.getElementById('task-edit-id').value = editId;
  document.getElementById('t-name').value = t.name;
  document.getElementById('t-priority').value = t.priority;
  document.getElementById('t-linked').value = t.linked || '';
  document.getElementById('t-date').value = t.date;
  document.getElementById('t-time').value = t.time || '09:00';
  document.getElementById('t-note').value = t.note || '';
  } else{
  title.textContent = '✅ إضافة مهمة جديدة';
  document.getElementById('task-edit-id').value = '';
  document.getElementById('t-name').value = '';
  document.getElementById('t-priority').value = 'medium';
  document.getElementById('t-linked').value = '';
  document.getElementById('t-note').value = '';
  setTaskDate(0);
  }
  openModal('m-add-task');
}
function setTaskDate(daysOffset){
  const d = new Date();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + daysOffset);
  document.getElementById('t-date').value = d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
}
function saveTask(){
  const name = document.getElementById('t-name').value.trim();
  const date = document.getElementById('t-date').value;
  if (!name){ showToast('⚠️ اسم المهمة مطلوب', '#D9534F'); return; }
  if (!date){ showToast('⚠️ التاريخ مطلوب', '#D9534F'); return; }
  if (!data.tasks) data.tasks = [];
  const editId = document.getElementById('task-edit-id').value;
  if (editId){
  const idx = data.tasks.findIndex(t => t.id == editId);
  if (idx >= 0){
  snapshot('تعديل مهمة: ' + name);
  data.tasks[idx] ={
  ...data.tasks[idx],
  name,
  priority: document.getElementById('t-priority').value,
  linked:   document.getElementById('t-linked').value,
  date,
  time:     document.getElementById('t-time').value,
  note:     document.getElementById('t-note').value.trim(),
  };
  }
  } else{
  snapshot('إضافة مهمة: ' + name);
  data.tasks.push({
  id:        Date.now(),
  name,
  priority:  document.getElementById('t-priority').value,
  linked:    document.getElementById('t-linked').value,
  date,
  time:      document.getElementById('t-time').value || '09:00',
  note:      document.getElementById('t-note').value.trim(),
  completed: false,
  createdAt: today()
  });
  }
  saveData();
  closeModal('m-add-task');
  renderTasks(currentTaskFilter);
  updateTaskStats();
  updateDashboardTaskWidget();
  showToast('✅ تم حفظ المهمة');
}
function renderTasks(filter){
  currentTaskFilter = filter || 'all';
  const grid = document.getElementById('tasks-container');
  if (!grid) return;
  ['all','pending','done','urgent','overdue'].forEach(p =>{
  const el = document.getElementById('tpill-' + p);
  if (el) el.classList.toggle('active', p === currentTaskFilter);
  });
  const todayStr = today();
  const search = (document.getElementById('t-search')?.value || '').toLowerCase();
  const tasks = data.tasks || [];
  let list = tasks;
  if (currentTaskFilter === 'pending')  list = list.filter(t => !t.completed);
  else if (currentTaskFilter === 'done') list = list.filter(t => t.completed);
  else if (currentTaskFilter === 'urgent') list = list.filter(t => t.priority === 'urgent' && !t.completed);
  else if (currentTaskFilter === 'overdue') list = list.filter(t => !t.completed && t.date < todayStr);
  if (search) list = list.filter(t => t.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) || (t.note||'').toLowerCase().includes(search));
  list = [...list].sort((a, b) =>{
  if (!a.completed && !b.completed){
  if (a.date < todayStr && b.date >= todayStr) return -1;
  if (b.date < todayStr && a.date >= todayStr) return 1;
  }
  return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);
  });
  if (!list.length){
  grid.innerHTML = '<div class="empty" style="grid-column:1/-1">لا توجد مهام</div>';
  return;
  }
  const priMap ={ urgent:{label:'عاجل جداً',color:'#D9534F',icon:'🔴'},
  medium:{label:'متوسط',     color:'#F9B264',icon:'🟡'},
  normal:{label:'عادي',       color:'#3b82f6',   icon:'🔵'} };
  const linkedMap ={ worker:'👷 عامل', sponsor:'🤝 كفيل',
  accounting:'📒 محاسبة', transfer:'📅 استحقاق', general:'📌 عام' };
  grid.innerHTML = list.map(t =>{
  const p = priMap[t.priority] || priMap.normal;
  const isOverdue = !t.completed && t.date < todayStr;
  const isToday   = !t.completed && t.date === todayStr;
  const bdrClr = isOverdue ? 'rgba(239,68,68,0.5)' : isToday ? 'rgba(245,158,11,0.4)' : 'var(--line)';
  const realIdx = data.tasks.indexOf(t);
  return `<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1.5px solid ${bdrClr};border-radius:14px;padding:16px;position:relative;overflow:hidden;transition:all .2s"
  onmouseover="this.style.transform='translateY(-2px)'" onmouseout="this.style.transform=''">
  <div style="position:absolute;top:0;right:0;width:4px;height:100%;background:${p.color};border-radius:0 14px 14px 0"></div>
  <div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:10px">
  <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:14px;color:${t.completed?'var(--ink3)':'var(--ink)'};text-decoration:${t.completed?'line-through':'none'};padding-left:8px;flex:1">${t.name}</div>
  <span style="background:rgba(${p.color.includes('red')?'239,68,68':p.color.includes('gold')?'201,168,76':'59,130,246'},0.12);color:${p.color};font-size:10px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px;white-space:nowrap">${p.icon} ${p.label}</span>
  </div>
  <div style="display:flex;gap:12px;font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3);margin-bottom:8px">
  <span>📅 ${t.date}</span>
  <span>⏰ ${t.time||'09:00'}</span>
  ${isOverdue ? '<span style="color:#ef4444;font-weight:700">⚠️ متأخرة</span>' : ''}
  ${isToday   ? '<span style="color:var(--accent2);font-weight:700">🟡 اليوم</span>' : ''}
  </div>
  ${t.linked ? `<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3);margin-bottom:8px">${linkedMap[t.linked]||t.linked}</div>` : ''}
  ${t.note ? `<div style="font-size:12px;color:var(--ink3);margin-bottom:10px;font-style:italic">${t.note}</div>` : ''}
  <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-top:10px">
  ${!t.completed
  ? `<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="flex:1;justify-content:center;font-size:11px" onclick="completeTask(${t.id})">✅ إنجاز</button>`
  : `<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="flex:1;justify-content:center;font-size:11px" onclick="restoreTask(${t.id})">↩️ إعادة</button>`
  }
  <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" style="font-size:11px;padding:0 10px" onclick="openTaskForm(${t.id})">✏️</button>
  <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" style="font-size:11px;padding:0 10px" onclick="deleteTask(${t.id})">🗑️</button>
  </div>
  </div>`;
  }).join('');
}
function completeTask(id){
  const t = (data.tasks||[]).find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!t) return;
  snapshot('إنجاز مهمة: ' + t.name);
  t.completed = true;
  saveData();
  renderTasks(currentTaskFilter);
  updateTaskStats();
  updateDashboardTaskWidget();
  showToast('✅ تم إنجاز المهمة!');
}
function restoreTask(id){
  const t = (data.tasks||[]).find(x => x.id === id);
  if (!t) return;
  snapshot('إعادة مهمة: ' + t.name);
  t.completed = false;
  saveData();
  renderTasks(currentTaskFilter);
  updateTaskStats();
  updateDashboardTaskWidget();
  showToast('↩️ تم إعادة المهمة');
}
function deleteTask(id){
  if (!confirm('حذف هذه المهمة؟')) return;
  snapshot('حذف مهمة');
  data.tasks = (data.tasks||[]).filter(t => t.id !== id);
  saveData();
  renderTasks(currentTaskFilter);
  updateTaskStats();
  updateDashboardTaskWidget();
  showToast('🗑️ تم الحذف');
}
function updateTaskStats(){
  const tasks = data.tasks || [];
  const total     = tasks.length;
  const done      = tasks.filter(t => t.completed).length;
  const pending   = total - done;
  const pct       = total ? Math.round(done / total * 100) : 0;
  const set = (id, v) =>{ const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.textContent=v; };
  set('t-stat-total',   total);
  set('t-stat-pending', pending);
  set('t-stat-done',    done);
  set('t-stat-pct',     pct + '%');
  const bar = document.getElementById('t-progress-bar');
  if (bar) bar.style.width = pct + '%';
}
function updateDashboardTaskWidget(){
  const widget = document.getElementById('dash-tasks-list');
  if (!widget) return;
  const tasks = data.tasks || [];
  const todayStr = today();
  const urgent = tasks.filter(t => !t.completed && t.priority === 'urgent').length;
  const overdue = tasks.filter(t => !t.completed && t.date < todayStr).length;
  const dueToday = tasks.filter(t => !t.completed && t.date === todayStr).length;
  const pending = tasks.filter(t => !t.completed).length;
  if (!pending && !overdue){ widget.style.display = 'none'; return; }
  widget.style.display = 'block';
  const recentTasks = tasks
  .filter(t => !t.completed && t.date <= todayStr)
  .sort((a,b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date))
  .slice(0, 5);
  const priMap ={ urgent:{label:'عاجل',color:'#ef4444'},medium:{label:'متوسط',color:'#F9B264'},normal:{label:'عادي',color:'#3b82f6'} };
  widget.innerHTML = `
  <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:var(--ink);margin-bottom:12px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
  <span style="font-size:18px">✅</span> المهام المعلقة
  ${overdue  ? `<span style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.15);color:#ef4444;font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px">${overdue} متأخرة</span>` : ''}
  ${dueToday ? `<span style="background:rgba(245,158,11,0.15);color:var(--accent2);font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px">${dueToday} اليوم</span>` : ''}
  ${urgent   ? `<span style="background:rgba(239,68,68,0.1);color:#ef4444;font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px">${urgent} عاجل</span>` : ''}
  <button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" style="margin-right:auto;height:26px;font-size:11px;padding:0 10px" onclick="showSec('tasks')">عرض الكل</button>
  </div>
  ${recentTasks.map(t =>{
  const p = priMap[t.priority]||priMap.normal;
  const isOver = t.date < todayStr;
  return `<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid ${isOver?'rgba(239,68,68,0.3)':'var(--line)'};border-radius:9px;padding:9px 13px;margin-bottom:6px">
  <div>
  <span style="font-weight:700;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink)">${t.name}</span>
  ${t.note ? `<span style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:11px;margin-right:8px">${t.note}</span>` : ''}
  </div>
  <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px">
  <span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">${t.date}</span>
  <span style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:${p.color}">${p.label}</span>
  <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" style="height:24px;font-size:10px;padding:0 8px" onclick="completeTask(${t.id})">✅</button>
  </div>
  </div>`;
  }).join('')}
  ${pending > 5 ? `<div style="text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:var(--ink3);padding:6px">و ${pending-5} مهمة أخرى...</div>` : ''}
  `;
}
const _origShowSec = showSec;
showSec = function(id){
  _origShowSec(id);
  if (id === 'tasks'){
  renderTasks(currentTaskFilter);
  updateTaskStats();
  }
};
setInterval(function(){
  const now = new Date();
  const timeStr = now.toTimeString().slice(0,5);
  const dateStr = now.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  (data.tasks||[]).forEach(t =>{
  if (!t.completed && t.date === dateStr && t.time === timeStr){
  showToast('🔔 حان وقت مهمة: ' + t.name, '#8b5cf6');
  }
  });
}, 60000);
function initTasksModule(){
  if (!data.tasks) data.tasks = [];
  updateTaskStats();
  updateDashboardTaskWidget();
}
const BACKUP_KEY     = 'hulul_backups';
const BACKUP_CFG_KEY = 'hulul_backup_cfg';
let   backupTimer    = null;
function getBackupConfig(){
  try{
  return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_CFG_KEY)) ||{ interval: 15, maxSlots: 5 };
  } catch{ return{ interval: 15, maxSlots: 5 }; }
}
function saveBackupConfig(cfg){
  localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_CFG_KEY, JSON.stringify(cfg));
}
function loadBackups(){
  try{
  return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BACKUP_KEY)) || [];
  } catch{ return []; }
}
function saveBackups(backups){
  try{
  localStorage.setItem(BACKUP_KEY, JSON.stringify(backups));
  } catch(e){
  showToast('⚠️ مساحة التخزين ممتلئة! احذف نسخاً قديمة.', 'var(--accent2)');
  }
}
function createBackupNow(label){
  const cfg      = getBackupConfig();
  const backups  = loadBackups();
  const now      = new Date();
  const ts       = now.toISOString();
  const dateStr  = now.toLocaleDateString('ar-SA',{ year:'numeric', month:'short', day:'numeric' });
  const timeStr  = now.toLocaleTimeString('ar-SA',{ hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit' });
  const stats ={
  workers:   (data.workers||[]).length,
  sponsors:  (data.sponsors||[]).length,
  sales:     (data.sales||[]).length,
  expenses:  (data.expensesList||[]).length,
  inventory: (data.inventory||[]).length,
  contacts:  (data.customers||[]).length,
  saudi:     (data.saudiEmployees||[]).length,
  };
  const entry ={
  id:        ts,
  label:     label || (dateStr + ' — ' + timeStr),
  timestamp: ts,
  dateStr,
  timeStr,
  auto:      !label,
  stats,
  dataSize:  Math.round(JSON.stringify(data).length / 1024),
  payload:   JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data))  // deep clone
  };
  backups.unshift(entry);
  while (backups.length > cfg.maxSlots) backups.pop();
  saveBackups(backups);
  updateBackupStatusBar();
  renderBackupList();
  const msg = label ? '✅ تم إنشاء النسخة: ' + label : '💾 نسخة احتياطية تلقائية محفوظة';
  showToast(msg, label ? '#F9B264' : '#10b981');
  return entry;
}
function restoreBackup(id){
  const backups = loadBackups();
  const entry   = backups.find(b => b.id === id);
  if (!entry){ showToast('⚠️ النسخة غير موجودة', '#ef4444'); return; }
  if (!confirm('هل أنت متأكد من استعادة النسخة الاحتياطية؟\nسيتم استبدال البيانات الحالية بالكامل.')) return;
  snapshot('استعادة نسخة: ' + entry.label);
  Object.assign(data, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(entry.payload)));
  saveData();
  updateHeaderStats();
  const active = document.querySelector('.nav-item.active');
  if (active){
  const sec = active.getAttribute('onclick')?.match(/'([^']+)'/)?.[1];
  if (sec) showSec(sec);
  }
  closeModal('m-backup');
  showToast('✅ تمت الاستعادة من: ' + entry.label, '#F9B264');
}
function deleteBackup(id){
  let backups = loadBackups();
  backups = backups.filter(b => b.id !== id);
  saveBackups(backups);
  renderBackupList();
  updateBackupStatusBar();
  showToast('🗑️ تم حذف النسخة');
}
function exportBackupFile(){
  const backups = loadBackups();
  const current ={
  id: new Date().toISOString(),
  label: 'تصدير يدوي — ' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('ar-SA'),
  timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
  payload: data,
  stats:{
  workers: (data.workers||[]).length,
  sales: (data.sales||[]).length,
  }
  };
  const exportData ={ system: 'نظام حلول', version: 'v4', exported: new Date().toISOString(), backup: current, allBackups: backups };
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2)],{ type: 'application/json' });
  const a    = document.createElement('a');
  a.href     = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = 'thiqa-backup-' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10) + '.json';
  a.click();
  showToast('📥 تم تصدير ملف النسخة الاحتياطية');
}
function importBackupFile(input){
  const file = input.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = e =>{
  try{
  const parsed = JSON.parse(e.target.result);
  const backup = parsed.backup || parsed;
  if (!backup.payload){ showToast('⚠️ ملف غير صالح', '#ef4444'); return; }
  if (!confirm('هل تريد استعادة البيانات من هذا الملف؟\nالتاريخ: ' + (backup.label || backup.timestamp || '—'))) return;
  snapshot('استيراد نسخة خارجية');
  Object.assign(data, JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(backup.payload)));
  saveData();
  updateHeaderStats();
  closeModal('m-backup');
  showToast('✅ تمت استعادة البيانات من الملف', '#F9B264');
  } catch(err){
  showToast('❌ خطأ في قراءة الملف: ' + err.message, '#ef4444');
  }
  };
  reader.readAsText(file);
  input.value = '';
}
function updateBackupSettings(){
  const interval = parseInt(document.getElementById('backup-interval')?.value || '15');
  const maxSlots = parseInt(document.getElementById('backup-max')?.value || '5');
  saveBackupConfig({ interval, maxSlots });
  startAutoBackup();
  showToast('⚙️ تم حفظ إعدادات النسخ الاحتياطي');
}
function startAutoBackup(){
  if (backupTimer) clearInterval(backupTimer);
  const cfg = getBackupConfig();
  if (cfg.interval > 0){
  backupTimer = setInterval(() => createBackupNow(), cfg.interval * 60 * 1000);
  }
  const intEl = document.getElementById('backup-interval');
  const maxEl = document.getElementById('backup-max');
  if (intEl) intEl.value = String(cfg.interval);
  if (maxEl) maxEl.value = String(cfg.maxSlots);
}
function updateBackupStatusBar(){
  const backups = loadBackups();
  const dot     = document.getElementById('backup-dot');
  const txt     = document.getElementById('backup-status-txt');
  if (!dot || !txt) return;
  if (!backups.length){
  dot.style.background = '#6b7280';
  txt.textContent = 'لا توجد نسخة احتياطية';
  return;
  }
  const last    = backups[0];
  const lastDate = new Date(last.timestamp);
  const diffMin  = Math.round((Date.now() - lastDate.getTime()) / 60000);
  let timeAgo;
  if (diffMin < 1)        timeAgo = 'الآن';
  else if (diffMin < 60)  timeAgo = 'منذ ' + diffMin + ' د';
  else if (diffMin < 1440)timeAgo = 'منذ ' + Math.round(diffMin/60) + ' س';
  else                    timeAgo = 'منذ ' + Math.round(diffMin/1440) + ' يوم';
  dot.style.background = diffMin < 30 ? '#10b981' : diffMin < 120 ? 'var(--accent2)' : '#ef4444';
  txt.textContent = '💾 ' + timeAgo + ' (' + backups.length + ' نسخ)';
}
function renderBackupList(){
  const backups   = loadBackups();
  const container = document.getElementById('backup-list-container');
  const countEl   = document.getElementById('backup-count-badge');
  if (!container) return;
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = backups.length;
  if (!backups.length){
  container.innerHTML = '<div class="empty" style="padding:30px;text-align:center;color:var(--ink3)">لا توجد نسخ احتياطية بعد<br><small>اضغط "إنشاء نسخة الآن" للبدء</small></div>';
  return;
  }
  container.innerHTML = backups.map(function(b){
  var isAuto = b.auto;
  var size   = b.dataSize || '—';
  var statsLine = [
  b.stats && b.stats.workers  ? '👷 ' + b.stats.workers  : null,
  b.stats && b.stats.sales    ? '💰 ' + b.stats.sales    : null,
  b.stats && b.stats.contacts ? '👤 ' + b.stats.contacts : null,
  ].filter(Boolean).join('  ');
  var typeBadge = isAuto
  ? '<span style="color:#6b7280">تلقائي</span>'
  : '<span style="color:var(--accent-dk)">يدوي</span>';
  return '<div style="background:var(--surface);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:10px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px">'
  + '<div style="font-size:24px;flex-shrink:0">' + (isAuto ? '🤖' : '📸') + '</div>'
  + '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0">'
  +   '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink);margin-bottom:3px">' + b.label + '</div>'
  +   '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">' + typeBadge + ' · ' + size + ' KB · ' + (statsLine || 'لا بيانات') + '</div>'
  + '</div>'
  + '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;flex-shrink:0">'
  +   '<button class="btn btn-gold btn-sm" onclick="restoreBackup(this.dataset.id)" data-id="' + b.id + '" title="استعادة">↩ استعادة</button>'
  +   '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="deleteBackup(this.dataset.id)" data-id="' + b.id + '" title="حذف" style="color:#ef4444;border-color:#ef4444">🗑️</button>'
  + '</div>'
  + '</div>';
  }).join('');
}
const _origOpenModal = openModal;
openModal = function(id){
  _origOpenModal(id);
  if(id==='m-add-worker'){ populateWorkerSponsorSelect(); populateSponsorSelects(); }
  if(id==='m-add-transfer') populateTransferWorkerSelect();
  if(id==='m-add-receipt'){
  const sel=document.getElementById('r-category');
  if(sel&&sel.options.length<=1&&data.categories){
  data.categories.forEach(c=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=c;o.textContent=c;sel.appendChild(o);});
  }
  }
  if(id==='m-add-entitlement'||id==='m-edit-entitlement'){
  populateBrokerSelects();
  const dd=document.getElementById('ent-due-date');
  if(dd&&!dd.value) dd.value=today();
  }
  if(id==='m-backup'){ renderBackupList(); updateBackupStatusBar(); }
  setTimeout(()=>{
  const modal=document.getElementById(id);
  if(modal) modal.querySelectorAll('input[type="date"]').forEach(i=>{if(!i.value)i.value=today();});
  },50);
};
;
var _origSaveData = typeof _origSaveData !== "undefined" ? _origSaveData : saveData;
saveData = function(){
  _origSaveData();
  updateBackupStatusBar();
};
const PAY_LABEL ={cash:'💵 نقداً', bank:'🏦 بنك', transfer:'📲 تحويل'};
function populateReceiptModal(){
  const catSel = document.getElementById('r-category');
  if (catSel) catSel.innerHTML = (data.categories||[]).map(c=>'<option>'+c+'</option>').join('');
  const fcat = document.getElementById('rec-filter-cat');
  if (fcat){
  fcat.innerHTML = '<option value="">كل التصنيفات</option>' +
  (data.categories||[]).map(c=>'<option>'+c+'</option>').join('');
  }
  const dl = document.getElementById('r-vendors-list');
  if (dl){
  const vs = [...new Set((data.receipts||[]).map(r=>r.vendor).filter(Boolean))];
  dl.innerHTML = vs.map(v=>'<option value="'+v+'">').join('');
  }
  const dt = document.getElementById('r-date');
  if (dt && !dt.value) dt.value = today();
}
function updateReceiptAmountColor(){
  const type = document.getElementById('r-type')?.value;
  const amt  = document.getElementById('r-amount');
  if (amt) amt.style.color = type==='income' ? '#10b981' : '#ef4444';
}
function saveReceipt(){
  const desc   = document.getElementById('r-desc')?.value.trim()||'';
  const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('r-amount')?.value)||0;
  const date   = document.getElementById('r-date')?.value||today();
  if (!desc){ showToast('⚠️ البيان مطلوب','#D9534F'); return; }
  if (!amount){ showToast('⚠️ المبلغ مطلوب','#D9534F'); return; }
  snapshot('إضافة إيصال');
  const receipt ={
  id:       Date.now(),
  type:     document.getElementById('r-type')?.value||'expense',
  date, category: document.getElementById('r-category')?.value||'أخرى',
  vendor:   document.getElementById('r-vendor')?.value.trim()||'',
  desc, amount,
  payment:  document.getElementById('r-payment')?.value||'cash',
  note:     document.getElementById('r-note')?.value.trim()||'',
  createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  if (!data.receipts) data.receipts=[];
  data.receipts.unshift(receipt);
  if (receipt.vendor && !(data.vendors||[]).includes(receipt.vendor)){
  if (!data.vendors) data.vendors=[];
  data.vendors.push(receipt.vendor);
  }
  addOp((receipt.type==='income'?'💰 وارد: ':'💸 منصرف: ')+receipt.desc+' — '+fmt(receipt.amount)+' ر.س');
  saveData();
  renderReceipts();
  closeModal('m-add-receipt');
  showToast((receipt.type==='income'?'✅ وارد: ':'✅ منصرف: ')+fmt(receipt.amount)+' ر.س');
  ['r-desc','r-vendor','r-amount','r-note'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
}
function deleteReceipt(id){
  if (!confirm('هل تريد حذف هذا الإيصال؟')) return;
  snapshot('حذف إيصال');
  data.receipts = data.receipts.filter(r=>r.id!==id);
  saveData(); renderReceipts();
  showToast('🗑️ تم الحذف');
}
function renderReceipts(){
  const tbody    = document.getElementById('receipts-tbody');
  if (!tbody) return;
  const search   = (document.getElementById('rec-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  const fType    = document.getElementById('rec-filter-type')?.value||'';
  const fCat     = document.getElementById('rec-filter-cat')?.value||'';
  const fPay     = document.getElementById('rec-filter-payment')?.value||'';
  const fFrom    = document.getElementById('rec-filter-from')?.value||'';
  const fTo      = document.getElementById('rec-filter-to')?.value||'';
  const all      = data.receipts||[];
  const list     = all.filter(r=>{
  if (fType && r.type!==fType) return false;
  if (fCat  && r.category!==fCat) return false;
  if (fPay  && r.payment!==fPay) return false;
  if (fFrom && r.date < fFrom) return false;
  if (fTo   && r.date > fTo)   return false;
  if (search && !r.desc?.toLowerCase().includes(search) &&
  !r.vendor?.toLowerCase().includes(search) &&
  !r.category?.toLowerCase().includes(search)) return false;
  return true;
  });
  let inc=0, exp=0;
  all.forEach(r=>r.type==='income'?inc+=r.amount||0:exp+=r.amount||0);
  const net=inc-exp;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('rec-total', all.length);
  set('rec-income', fmt(inc)+' ر.س');
  set('rec-expense', fmt(exp)+' ر.س');
  set('rec-net', (net>=0?'+':'')+fmt(net)+' ر.س');
  set('receiptsBadge', all.length);
  const netEl=document.getElementById('rec-net');
  if(netEl) netEl.style.color=net>=0?'#10b981':'#ef4444';
  if (!list.length){ tbody.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="9" class="empty">لا توجد إيصالات</td></tr>'; return; }
  tbody.innerHTML = list.map((r,i)=>{
  const isInc = r.type==='income';
  const badge = isInc
  ? '<span class="badge badge-teal">💰 وارد</span>'
  : '<span class="badge badge-red">💸 منصرف</span>';
  return '<tr>'
  +'<td>'+(i+1)+'</td>'
  +'<td>'+(r.date||'—')+'</td>'
  +'<td>'+badge+'</td>'
  +'<td><span style="background:rgba(235,163,62,0.12);color:var(--accent-dk);padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px">'+(r.category||'—')+'</span></td>'
  +'<td>'+(r.vendor||'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">—</span>')+'</td>'
  +'<td style="max-width:160px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap" title="'+(r.desc||'')+'">'+(r.desc||'—')+'</td>'
  +'<td>'+(PAY_LABEL[r.payment]||'—')+'</td>'
  +'<td style="font-weight:800;color:'+(isInc?'#10b981':'#ef4444')+'">'+fmt(r.amount)+' ر.س</td>'
  +'<td><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="deleteReceipt('+r.id+')">🗑️</button></td>'
  +'</tr>';
  }).join('');
  const fcat = document.getElementById('rec-filter-cat');
  if (fcat && fcat.options.length<=1)
  (data.categories||[]).forEach(c=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=c;o.textContent=c;fcat.appendChild(o);});
}
function renderReceiptsReport(prefix){
  const all   = data.receipts||[];
  let inc=0, exp=0;
  all.forEach(r=>r.type==='income'?inc+=r.amount||0:exp+=r.amount||0);
  const net=inc-exp;
  const set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set(prefix+'-total',   all.length);
  set(prefix+'-income',  fmt(inc)+' ر.س');
  set(prefix+'-expense', fmt(exp)+' ر.س');
  set(prefix+'-net',     (net>=0?'+':'')+fmt(net)+' ر.س');
  const netEl=document.getElementById(prefix+'-net');
  if(netEl) netEl.style.color=net>=0?'#10b981':'#ef4444';
  const byCat={};
  all.forEach(r=>{
  if(!byCat[r.category]) byCat[r.category]={inc:0,exp:0};
  r.type==='income'?byCat[r.category].inc+=r.amount||0:byCat[r.category].exp+=r.amount||0;
  });
  const catsEl=document.getElementById(prefix+'-report-cats')||(prefix==='rec'?document.getElementById('rec-report-cats'):null);
  if(catsEl){
  const cats=Object.entries(byCat).sort((a,b)=>(b[1].inc+b[1].exp)-(a[1].inc+a[1].exp));
  const maxV=cats.length?Math.max(...cats.map(([,v])=>v.inc+v.exp)):1;
  catsEl.innerHTML=cats.map(([cat,v])=>{
  const tot=v.inc+v.exp; const pct=Math.round(tot/maxV*100);
  return '<div style="margin-bottom:10px">'
  +'<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:3px">'
  +'<span style="font-weight:600">'+cat+'</span>'
  +'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">'+fmt(tot)+' ر.س</span></div>'
  +'<div style="background:var(--line);border-radius:4px;height:6px;overflow:hidden">'
  +'<div style="width:'+pct+'%;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--accent),#e8c56b);border-radius:4px"></div>'
  +'</div></div>';
  }).join('') || '<div style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:12px">لا توجد بيانات</div>';
  }
  const byPay={};
  all.forEach(r=>{const k=PAY_LABEL[r.payment]||r.payment||'—';if(!byPay[k])byPay[k]=0;byPay[k]+=r.amount||0;});
  const payEl=document.getElementById(prefix+'-report-payments')||(prefix==='rec'?document.getElementById('rec-report-payments'):null);
  if(payEl){
  const pays=Object.entries(byPay).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
  const maxP=pays.length?Math.max(...pays.map(([,v])=>v)):1;
  payEl.innerHTML=pays.map(([pay,v])=>{
  const pct=Math.round(v/maxP*100);
  return '<div style="margin-bottom:10px">'
  +'<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:12px;margin-bottom:3px">'
  +'<span style="font-weight:600">'+pay+'</span>'
  +'<span style="color:var(--ink3)">'+fmt(v)+' ر.س</span></div>'
  +'<div style="background:var(--line);border-radius:4px;height:6px;overflow:hidden">'
  +'<div style="width:'+pct+'%;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#3b82f6,#60a5fa);border-radius:4px"></div>'
  +'</div></div>';
  }).join('') || '<div style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:12px">لا توجد بيانات</div>';
  }
  const byMonth={};
  all.forEach(r=>{
  const m=(r.date||'').slice(0,7);
  if(!m)return;
  if(!byMonth[m])byMonth[m]={inc:0,exp:0,count:0};
  r.type==='income'?byMonth[m].inc+=r.amount||0:byMonth[m].exp+=r.amount||0;
  byMonth[m].count++;
  });
  const monthEl=document.getElementById(prefix+'-monthly')||(prefix==='rec'?document.getElementById('rec-report-monthly'):null);
  if(monthEl){
  const months=Object.entries(byMonth).sort((a,b)=>b[0].localeCompare(a[0]));
  monthEl.innerHTML=months.map(([m,v])=>{
  const net=v.inc-v.exp;
  return '<tr>'
  +'<td style="font-weight:700">'+m+'</td>'
  +'<td style="color:#10b981;font-weight:700">'+fmt(v.inc)+' ر.س</td>'
  +'<td style="color:#ef4444;font-weight:700">'+fmt(v.exp)+' ر.س</td>'
  +'<td style="font-weight:800;color:'+(net>=0?'#10b981':'#ef4444')+'">'+(net>=0?'+':'')+fmt(net)+' ر.س</td>'
  +'<td>'+v.count+'</td>'
  +'</tr>';
  }).join('') || '<tr><td colspan="5" class="empty">لا توجد بيانات</td></tr>';
  }
}
function exportReceipts(){
  const list=data.receipts||[];
  if(!list.length){showToast('⚠️ لا توجد إيصالات','#D9534F');return;}
  const header='التاريخ,النوع,التصنيف,المورد,البيان,طريقة الدفع,المبلغ,الملاحظة\n';
  const rows=list.map(r=>[r.date,r.type==='income'?'وارد':'منصرف',r.category,r.vendor||'',r.desc,r.payment,r.amount,r.note||''].join(',')).join('\n');
  const blob=new Blob(['\uFEFF'+header+rows],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8'});
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);a.download='receipts-'+today()+'.csv';a.click();
  showToast('📥 تم تصدير '+list.length+' إيصال');
}
;
const ENT_STATUS ={
  paid:{ label:'✅ مدفوع',     color:'#10b981', bg:'rgba(16,185,129,0.12)' },
  unpaid:{ label:'⏳ غير مدفوع', color:'var(--accent2)', bg:'rgba(245,158,11,0.12)' },
  late:{ label:'🔴 متأخر',     color:'#ef4444', bg:'rgba(239,68,68,0.12)'  }
};
function getBrokerName(id){
  const b = (data.brokers||[]).find(b => b.id === id);
  return b ? b.name : '—';
}
function autoUpdateLateStatus(){
  const t = today();
  let changed = false;
  (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).forEach(e =>{
  if (e.status === 'unpaid' && e.dueDate < t){
  e.status = 'late'; changed = true;
  }
  });
  if (changed) saveData();
}
function updateBrokerAlertBadge(){
  autoUpdateLateStatus();
  const t = today();
  const soon = new Date(); soon.setDate(soon.getDate() + 7);
  const soonStr = soon.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const alerts = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).filter(e =>
  e.status === 'late' || (e.status === 'unpaid' && e.dueDate <= soonStr)
  );
  const badge = document.getElementById('bk-alert-badge');
  if (badge){
  if (alerts.length){ badge.textContent = alerts.length; badge.style.display = 'inline-block'; }
  else{ badge.style.display = 'none'; }
  }
}
function populateBrokerSelects(){
  const brokers = data.brokers||[];
  ['ent-broker-id','edit-ent-broker-id','bk-sch-broker'].forEach(selId =>{
  const sel = document.getElementById(selId);
  if (!sel) return;
  const cur = sel.value;
  const isFilter = selId === 'bk-sch-broker';
  sel.innerHTML = (isFilter ? '<option value="">كل الوسطاء</option>' : '') +
  brokers.map(b => '<option value="' + b.id + '">' + b.name + '</option>').join('');
  if (cur) sel.value = cur;
  });
}
function addBroker(){
  const name = (document.getElementById('bk-name')?.value||'').trim();
  if (!name){ showToast('⚠️ اسم الوسيط مطلوب','#D9534F'); return; }
  const exists = (data.brokers||[]).find(b => b.name === name);
  if (exists){ showToast('⚠️ وسيط بهذا الاسم موجود مسبقاً','var(--accent2)'); return; }
  snapshot('إضافة وسيط');
  if (!data.brokers) data.brokers = [];
  const broker ={
  id:        Date.now(),
  name,
  phone:     (document.getElementById('bk-phone')?.value||'').trim(),
  nid:       (document.getElementById('bk-nid')?.value||'').trim(),
  email:     (document.getElementById('bk-email')?.value||'').trim(),
  specialty: (document.getElementById('bk-specialty')?.value||'').trim(),
  note:      (document.getElementById('bk-note')?.value||'').trim(),
  createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  data.brokers.push(broker);
  addOp('➕ وسيط جديد: ' + name);
  saveData();
  renderBrokers();
  populateBrokerSelects();
  closeModal('m-add-broker');
  showToast('✅ تم إضافة الوسيط: ' + name);
  ['bk-name','bk-phone','bk-nid','bk-email','bk-specialty','bk-note']
  .forEach(id =>{ const el=document.getElementById(id); if(el) el.value=''; });
}
function deleteBroker(id){
  const b = (data.brokers||[]).find(x => x.id===id);
  if (!b) return;
  const hasEnts = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).some(e => e.brokerId===id);
  if (hasEnts && !confirm('هذا الوسيط لديه مستحقات مرتبطة. هل تريد حذفه مع مستحقاته؟')) return;
  if (!hasEnts && !confirm('هل تريد حذف الوسيط: ' + b.name + '؟')) return;
  snapshot('حذف وسيط');
  data.brokers = data.brokers.filter(x => x.id!==id);
  if (hasEnts) data.brokerEntitlements = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).filter(e => e.brokerId!==id);
  addOp('🗑️ حذف وسيط: ' + b.name);
  saveData();
  renderBrokers();
  renderBrokerEntitlements();
  populateBrokerSelects();
  updateBrokerAlertBadge();
  showToast('🗑️ تم حذف الوسيط');
}
function renderBrokers(){
  const search = (document.getElementById('bk-search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();
  const list   = (data.brokers||[]).filter(b =>
  !search || b.name.toLowerCase().includes(search) || (b.specialty||'').toLowerCase().includes(search)
  );
  const grid = document.getElementById('brokers-tbody');
  if (!grid) return;
  const all_ents = data.brokerEntitlements||[];
  const totalSum = all_ents.reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.amount||0),0);
  const paidSum  = all_ents.filter(e=>e.status==='paid').reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.amount||0),0);
  const unpaidSum= all_ents.filter(e=>e.status==='unpaid').reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.amount||0),0);
  const lateSum  = all_ents.filter(e=>e.status==='late').reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.amount||0),0);
  const set=(id,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.textContent=v;};
  set('bk-total',  data.brokers.length);
  set('bk-sum',    fmt(totalSum)+' ر.س');
  set('bk-paid',   fmt(paidSum)+' ر.س');
  set('bk-unpaid', fmt(unpaidSum)+' ر.س');
  set('bk-late',   fmt(lateSum)+' ر.س');
  renderBrokerAlertsBox();
  if (!list.length){
  grid.innerHTML = '<div style="grid-column:1/-1;text-align:center;padding:40px;color:var(--ink3)">لا يوجد وسطاء — اضغط "+ إضافة وسيط"</div>';
  return;
  }
  grid.innerHTML = list.map(b =>{
  const ents      = all_ents.filter(e => e.brokerId===b.id);
  const bTotal    = ents.reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.amount||0),0);
  const bPaid     = ents.filter(e=>e.status==='paid').length;
  const bUnpaid   = ents.filter(e=>e.status==='unpaid').length;
  const bLate     = ents.filter(e=>e.status==='late').length;
  const nextDue   = ents.filter(e=>e.status!=='paid').sort((a,b)=>a.dueDate<b.dueDate?-1:1)[0];
  return '<div class="card" style="padding:0;overflow:hidden">'
  + '<div style="padding:14px 16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--surface),var(--bg));border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px">'
  +   '<div style="width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),rgba(235,163,62,0.5));display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:18px;font-weight:900;color:#0D1929;flex-shrink:0">'
  +     b.name.charAt(0)
  +   '</div>'
  +   '<div style="flex:1;min-width:0">'
  +     '<div style="font-weight:800;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink)">' + b.name + '</div>'
  +     '<div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--ink3)">' + (b.specialty||'وسيط') + (b.phone?' · '+b.phone:'') + '</div>'
  +   '</div>'
  +   '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="openAddEntitlementFor(' + b.id + ')" style="font-size:10px">+ مستحق</button>'
  + '</div>'
  + '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)">'
  +   '<div style="padding:10px;text-align:center;border-left:1px solid var(--line)">'
  +     '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ink3)">مدفوع</div>'
  +     '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#10b981">' + bPaid + '</div>'
  +   '</div>'
  +   '<div style="padding:10px;text-align:center;border-left:1px solid var(--line)">'
  +     '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ink3)">غير مدفوع</div>'
  +     '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:var(--accent2)">' + bUnpaid + '</div>'
  +   '</div>'
  +   '<div style="padding:10px;text-align:center">'
  +     '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ink3)">متأخر</div>'
  +     '<div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#ef4444">' + bLate + '</div>'
  +   '</div>'
  + '</div>'
  + '<div style="padding:12px 16px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between">'
  +   '<div>'
  +     '<div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ink3)">إجمالي المستحقات</div>'
  +     '<div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:var(--accent-dk)">' + fmt(bTotal) + ' ر.س</div>'
  +   '</div>'
  +   (nextDue ? '<div style="text-align:left"><div style="font-size:10px;color:var(--ink3)">أقرب استحقاق</div><div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:' + (ENT_STATUS[nextDue.status]?.color||'var(--ink3)') + '">' + nextDue.dueDate + '</div></div>' : '')
  + '</div>'
  + '<div style="padding:10px 16px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end">'
  +   '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="viewBrokerDetails(' + b.id + ')">📋 المستحقات</button>'
  +   '<button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="deleteBroker(' + b.id + ')">🗑️</button>'
  + '</div>'
  + '</div>';
  }).join('');
}
function openAddEntitlementFor(brokerId){
  openModal('m-add-entitlement');
  setTimeout(() =>{
  const sel = document.getElementById('ent-broker-id');
  if (sel) sel.value = String(brokerId);
  }, 50);
}
function viewBrokerDetails(brokerId){
  switchBrokerTab('schedule');
  setTimeout(() =>{
  const sel = document.getElementById('bk-sch-broker');
  if (sel){ sel.value = String(brokerId); renderBrokerEntitlements(); }
  }, 50);
}
function saveEntitlement(){
  const brokerId = parseInt(document.getElementById('ent-broker-id')?.value||'0');
  const amount   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ent-amount')?.value||'0');
  const dueDate  = document.getElementById('ent-due-date')?.value||'';
  if (!brokerId){ showToast('⚠️ اختر الوسيط','#D9534F'); return; }
  if (!amount){ showToast('⚠️ المبلغ مطلوب','#D9534F'); return; }
  if (!dueDate){ showToast('⚠️ تاريخ الاستحقاق مطلوب','#D9534F'); return; }
  snapshot('إضافة مستحق');
  if (!data.brokerEntitlements) data.brokerEntitlements = [];
  const status = document.getElementById('ent-status')?.value || 'unpaid';
  const ent ={
  id:        Date.now(),
  brokerId,
  type:      document.getElementById('ent-type')?.value     || 'عمولة',
  amount,
  dueDate,
  status:    (status==='unpaid' && dueDate < today()) ? 'late' : status,
  note:      (document.getElementById('ent-note')?.value||'').trim(),
  createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  data.brokerEntitlements.push(ent);
  const bName = getBrokerName(brokerId);
  addOp('💰 مستحق جديد: ' + ent.type + ' — ' + bName + ' — ' + fmt(amount) + ' ر.س');
  saveData();
  renderBrokers();
  renderBrokerEntitlements();
  updateBrokerAlertBadge();
  closeModal('m-add-entitlement');
  showToast('✅ تم إضافة المستحق: ' + fmt(amount) + ' ر.س — ' + bName);
  ['ent-amount','ent-note'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
}
function openEditEntitlement(id){
  const e = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
  if (!e) return;
  populateBrokerSelects();
  const set=(eid,v)=>{const el=document.getElementById(eid);if(el)el.value=v;};
  set('edit-ent-id',       e.id);
  set('edit-ent-broker-id',e.brokerId);
  set('edit-ent-type',     e.type);
  set('edit-ent-amount',   e.amount);
  set('edit-ent-due-date', e.dueDate);
  set('edit-ent-status',   e.status);
  set('edit-ent-note',     e.note||'');
  openModal('m-edit-entitlement');
}
function updateEntitlement(){
  const id      = parseInt(document.getElementById('edit-ent-id')?.value||'0');
  const e       = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
  if (!e) return;
  snapshot('تعديل مستحق');
  const dueDate = document.getElementById('edit-ent-due')?.value||e.dueDate;
  const status  = document.getElementById('edit-ent-status')?.value||e.status;
  e.brokerId = parseInt(document.getElementById('edit-ent-broker-id')?.value||e.brokerId);
  e.type     = document.getElementById('edit-ent-type')?.value     || e.type;
  e.amount   = parseFloat(document.getElementById('edit-ent-amount')?.value||e.amount);
  e.dueDate  = dueDate;
  e.status   = (status==='unpaid' && dueDate < today()) ? 'late' : status;
  e.note     = (document.getElementById('edit-ent-note')?.value||'').trim();
  e.updatedAt= new Date().toISOString();
  addOp('✏️ تعديل مستحق: ' + e.type + ' — ' + getBrokerName(e.brokerId));
  saveData();
  renderBrokers();
  renderBrokerEntitlements();
  updateBrokerAlertBadge();
  closeModal('m-edit-entitlement');
  showToast('✅ تم تعديل المستحق');
}
function deleteEntitlement(id){
  const e = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
  if (!e || !confirm('حذف هذا المستحق؟')) return;
  snapshot('حذف مستحق');
  data.brokerEntitlements = data.brokerEntitlements.filter(x=>x.id!==id);
  addOp('🗑️ حذف مستحق: ' + e.type + ' — ' + getBrokerName(e.brokerId));
  saveData();
  renderBrokers();
  renderBrokerEntitlements();
  updateBrokerAlertBadge();
  showToast('🗑️ تم حذف المستحق');
}
function markEntitlementPaid(id){
  const e = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
  if (!e) return;
  snapshot('تحديث حالة مستحق');
  e.status = 'paid';
  e.paidAt = today();
  addOp('✅ دفع مستحق: ' + e.type + ' — ' + getBrokerName(e.brokerId));
  saveData();
  renderBrokers();
  renderBrokerEntitlements();
  updateBrokerAlertBadge();
  showToast('✅ تم تحديد المستحق كمدفوع');
}
function renderBrokerEntitlements(){
  autoUpdateLateStatus();
  populateBrokerSelects();
  const tbody  = document.getElementById('entitlements-tbody');
  if (!tbody) return;
  const fBroker = document.getElementById('bk-sch-broker')?.value||'';
  const fStatus = document.getElementById('bk-sch-status')?.value||'';
  const fFrom   = document.getElementById('bk-sch-from')?.value||'';
  const fTo     = document.getElementById('bk-sch-to')?.value||'';
  const list = (data.brokerEntitlements||[])
  .filter(e =>{
  if (fBroker && String(e.brokerId) !== fBroker) return false;
  if (fStatus && e.status !== fStatus) return false;
  if (fFrom   && e.dueDate < fFrom)    return false;
  if (fTo     && e.dueDate > fTo)      return false;
  return true;
  })
  .sort((a,b) => a.dueDate < b.dueDate ? -1 : 1); // أقدم أولاً
  if (!list.length){
  tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:30px;color:var(--ink3)">لا توجد مستحقات مطابقة</td></tr>';
  return;
  }
  tbody.innerHTML = list.map((e, i) =>{
  const st  = ENT_STATUS[e.status] || ENT_STATUS.unpaid;
  const bName = getBrokerName(e.brokerId);
  return '<tr>'
  + '<td>' + (i+1) + '</td>'
  + '<td style="font-weight:700">' + bName + '</td>'
  + '<td><span style="background:var(--gold-bg,rgba(235,163,62,0.1));color:var(--accent-dk);padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px">' + e.type + '</span></td>'
  + '<td style="font-weight:800;color:var(--accent-dk)">' + fmt(e.amount) + ' ر.س</td>'
  + '<td>' + e.dueDate + '</td>'
  + '<td><span style="background:' + st.bg + ';color:' + st.color + ';padding:3px 10px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700">' + st.label + '</span></td>'
  + '<td style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:12px">' + (e.note||'—') + '</td>'
  + '<td>'
  +   '<div style="display:flex;gap:4px">'
  +     (e.status!=='paid' ? '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="color:#10b981;border-color:#10b981;font-size:10px;padding:3px 8px" onclick="markEntitlementPaid(' + e.id + ')">✅ دفع</button>' : '')
  +     '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="font-size:10px;padding:3px 8px" onclick="openEditEntitlement(' + e.id + ')">✏️</button>'
  +     '<button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" style="font-size:10px;padding:3px 8px" onclick="deleteEntitlement(' + e.id + ')">🗑️</button>'
  +   '</div>'
  + '</td>'
  + '</tr>';
  }).join('');
}
function renderOverdueTab(){
  const container = document.getElementById('bk-pane-overdue');
  if (!container) return;
  autoUpdateLateStatus();
  const late = (data.brokerEntitlements||[])
  .filter(e => e.status === 'late')
  .sort((a,b) => a.dueDate < b.dueDate ? -1 : 1);
  if (!late.length){
  container.innerHTML = '<div style="text-align:center;padding:50px;color:var(--ink3)">🎉 لا توجد مستحقات متأخرة</div>';
  return;
  }
  const byBroker ={};
  late.forEach(e =>{
  if (!byBroker[e.brokerId]) byBroker[e.brokerId] = [];
  byBroker[e.brokerId].push(e);
  });
  container.innerHTML = Object.entries(byBroker).map(([bid, ents]) =>{
  const bName = getBrokerName(parseInt(bid));
  const total = ents.reduce((s,e)=>s+(e.amount||0),0);
  const rows  = ents.map((e,i) =>
  '<tr>'
  + '<td>' + (i+1) + '</td>'
  + '<td><span style="background:rgba(235,163,62,0.1);color:var(--accent-dk);padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;font-size:11px">' + e.type + '</span></td>'
  + '<td style="font-weight:800;color:#ef4444">' + fmt(e.amount) + ' ر.س</td>'
  + '<td style="color:#ef4444;font-weight:700">' + e.dueDate + '</td>'
  + '<td style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:11px">' + (e.note||'—') + '</td>'
  + '<td><div style="display:flex;gap:4px">'
  +   '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="color:#10b981;border-color:#10b981;font-size:10px;padding:3px 8px" onclick="markEntitlementPaid(' + e.id + ')">✅ دفع</button>'
  +   '<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="font-size:10px;padding:3px 8px" onclick="openEditEntitlement(' + e.id + ')">✏️</button>'
  + '</div></td></tr>'
  ).join('');
  return '<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:14px;padding:0;overflow:hidden;border-color:rgba(239,68,68,0.3)">'
  + '<div style="padding:12px 16px;background:rgba(239,68,68,0.06);border-bottom:1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.2);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between">'
  +   '<div style="font-weight:800;color:var(--ink)">' + bName + '</div>'
  +   '<span style="color:#ef4444;font-weight:800;font-size:13px">إجمالي المتأخر: ' + fmt(total) + ' ر.س</span>'
  + '</div>'
  + '<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="data-table" style="width:100%">'
  + '<thead><tr><th>#</th><th>النوع</th><th>المبلغ</th><th>تاريخ الاستحقاق</th><th>ملاحظات</th><th>إجراءات</th></tr></thead>'
  + '<tbody>' + rows + '</tbody></table></div></div>';
  }).join('');
}
function renderBrokerAlertsBox(){
  const box = document.getElementById('bk-alerts-box');
  if (!box) return;
  autoUpdateLateStatus();
  const t = today();
  const soon = new Date(); soon.setDate(soon.getDate() + 7);
  const soonStr = soon.toISOString().slice(0,10);
  const late     = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).filter(e => e.status==='late');
  const upcoming = (data.brokerEntitlements||[]).filter(e => e.status==='unpaid' && e.dueDate > t && e.dueDate <= soonStr);
  if (!late.length && !upcoming.length){ box.style.display='none'; return; }
  box.style.display = 'block';
  const makeItem = (e, isLate) =>{
  const clr = isLate ? '#ef4444' : 'var(--accent2)';
  const lbl = isLate ? '🔴 متأخر' : '🟡 خلال 7 أيام';
  return '<div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;background:var(--surface);border:1px solid rgba('+(isLate?'239,68,68':'245,158,11')+',0.2);border-radius:8px;padding:9px 13px;margin-bottom:6px">'
  + '<div><span style="font-weight:800;color:var(--ink)">' + getBrokerName(e.brokerId) + '</span>'
  + '<span style="color:var(--ink3);font-size:11px;margin:0 8px">·</span>'
  + '<span style="color:'+clr+';font-size:12px">' + e.type + ' — ' + fmt(e.amount) + ' ر.س</span></div>'
